# CLAUDE.md

Behavioral guidelines to reduce common LLM coding mistakes. Merge with project-specific instructions as needed.

**Tradeoff:** These guidelines bias toward caution over speed. For trivial tasks, use judgment.

## 1. Think Before Coding

**Don't assume. Don't hide confusion. Surface tradeoffs.**

Before implementing:
- State your assumptions explicitly. If uncertain, ask.
- If multiple interpretations exist, present them - don't pick silently.
- If a simpler approach exists, say so. Push back when warranted.
- If something is unclear, stop. Name what's confusing. Ask.

## 2. Simplicity First

**Minimum code that solves the problem. Nothing speculative.**

- No features beyond what was asked.
- No abstractions for single-use code.
- No "flexibility" or "configurability" that wasn't requested.
- No error handling for impossible scenarios.
- If you write 200 lines and it could be 50, rewrite it.

Ask yourself: "Would a senior engineer say this is overcomplicated?" If yes, simplify.

## 3. Surgical Changes

**Touch only what you must. Clean up only your own mess.**

When editing existing code:
- Don't "improve" adjacent code, comments, or formatting.
- Don't refactor things that aren't broken.
- Match existing style, even if you'd do it differently.
- If you notice unrelated dead code, mention it - don't delete it.

When your changes create orphans:
- Remove imports/variables/functions that YOUR changes made unused.
- Don't remove pre-existing dead code unless asked.

The test: Every changed line should trace directly to the user's request.

## 4. Goal-Driven Execution

**Define success criteria. Loop until verified.**

Transform tasks into verifiable goals:
- "Add validation" → "Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass"
- "Fix the bug" → "Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass"
- "Refactor X" → "Ensure tests pass before and after"

For multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:
```
1. [Step] → verify: [check]
2. [Step] → verify: [check]
3. [Step] → verify: [check]
```

Strong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria ("make it work") require constant clarification.

---

**These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes.

+    timestamp: datetime
+    open: float
+    high: float
+    low: float
+    close: float
+    volume: int = Field(default=0, ge=0)
+
+
+class OHLCVBarResponse(BaseModel):
+    id: uuid.UUID
+    instrument_id: uuid.UUID
+    timeframe: str
+    timestamp: datetime
+    open: float
+    high: float
+    low: float
+    close: float
+    volume: int
+    created_at: datetime
+
+    model_config = {"from_attributes": True}
+
+
+# --- Strategy ---
+class StrategyCreate(BaseModel):
+    tenant_id: uuid.UUID | None = None
+    name: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=255)
+    description: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=1000)
+    version: str = Field(default="1.0.0", max_length=20)
+    owner_type: str = Field(default="PLATFORM", max_length=10)
+    code_reference: str | None = Field(default=None, max_length=500)
+
+
+class StrategyUpdate(BaseModel):
+    name: str | None = None
+    description: str | None = None
+    version: str | None = None
+    status: str | None = None
+    code_reference: str | None = None
+
+
+class StrategyResponse(BaseModel):
+    id: uuid.UUID
+    tenant_id: uuid.UUID | None
+    name: str
+    description: str | None
+    version: str
+    owner_type: str
+    status: str
+    code_reference: str | None
+    created_at: datetime
+    updated_at: datetime
+
+    model_config = {"from_attributes": True}
+
+
+# --- Strategy Config ---
+class StrategyConfigCreate(BaseModel):
+    portfolio_id: uuid.UUID
+    strategy_id: uuid.UUID
+    parameters: dict = Field(default_factory=dict)
+    lifecycle_status: str = Field(default="DEVELOPMENT", max_length=30)
+
+
+class StrategyConfigUpdate(BaseModel):
+    parameters: dict | None = None
+    lifecycle_status: str | None = None
+    is_active: bool | None = None
+
+
+class StrategyConfigResponse(BaseModel):

+    portfolio_id: uuid.UUID
+    strategy_id: uuid.UUID
+    parameters: dict
+    lifecycle_status: str
+    is_active: bool
+    created_at: datetime
+    updated_at: datetime
+
+    model_config = {"from_attributes": True}
+
+
+# --- Backtest ---
+class BacktestRunCreate(BaseModel):

+    strategy_config_id: uuid.UUID
+    start_date: date
+    end_date: date
+    timeframe: str = Field(default="1d", max_length=5)
+    parameters: dict = Field(default_factory=dict)
+
+
+class BacktestRunResponse(BaseModel):
+    id: uuid.UUID
+    tenant_id: uuid.UUID
+    portfolio_id: uuid.UUID
+    strategy_config_id: uuid.UUID
+    status: str
+    start_date: date
+    end_date: date
+    timeframe: str
+    parameters: dict
+    metrics: dict | None
+    error_message: str | None
+    created_at: datetime
+    completed_at: datetime | None
+
+    model_config = {"from_attributes": True}

### services/api-gateway/app/routers/instruments.py
     1	"""Instrument management endpoints."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import get_current_user, require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import Instrument, User
    14	from app.schemas import InstrumentCreate, InstrumentResponse
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/instruments", tags=["instruments"])
    17	
    18	
    19	@router.get("", response_model=list[InstrumentResponse])
    20	def list_instruments(
    21	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    22	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    23	):
    24	    """List all active instruments. Any authenticated user can view."""
    25	    return (
    26	        db.query(Instrument)
    27	        .filter(Instrument.is_active.is_(True))
    28	        .order_by(Instrument.symbol)
    29	        .all()
    30	    )
    31	
    32	
    33	@router.post("", response_model=InstrumentResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    34	def create_instrument(
    35	    body: InstrumentCreate,
    36	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    37	    current_user: User = Depends(require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN")),
    38	):
    39	    """Create a new instrument. Platform admin only."""
    40	    existing = (
    41	        db.query(Instrument)
    42	        .filter(Instrument.symbol == body.symbol, Instrument.exchange == body.exchange)
    43	        .first()
    44	    )
    45	    if existing:
    46	        raise HTTPException(
    47	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
    48	            detail=f"Instrument {body.symbol}:{body.exchange} already exists",
    49	        )
    50	
    51	    instrument = Instrument(
    52	        symbol=body.symbol,
    53	        exchange=body.exchange,
    54	        instrument_type=body.instrument_type,
    55	        name=body.name,
    56	        lot_size=body.lot_size,
    57	        tick_size=body.tick_size,
    58	    )
    59	    db.add(instrument)
    60	    db.flush()
    61	
    62	    record_audit(
    63	        db,
    64	        action="instrument_created",
    65	        user_id=current_user.id,
    66	        resource=f"instrument:{instrument.id}",
    67	        after_state=body.model_dump(),
    68	    )
    69	    db.commit()
    70	    db.refresh(instrument)
    71	    return instrument
    72	
    73	
    74	@router.get("/{instrument_id}", response_model=InstrumentResponse)
    75	def get_instrument(
    76	    instrument_id: uuid.UUID,
    77	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    78	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    79	):
    80	    """Get a single instrument by ID. Any authenticated user can view."""
    81	    instrument = db.query(Instrument).filter(Instrument.id == instrument_id).first()
    82	    if not instrument:
    83	        raise HTTPException(
    84	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Instrument not found"
    85	        )
    86	    return instrument
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/ohlcv.py
     1	"""OHLCV market data endpoints (nested under instruments)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	from datetime import datetime
     7	
     8	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
     9	from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert
    10	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    11	
    12	from app.auth import get_current_user, require_role
    13	from app.database import get_db
    14	from app.models import Instrument, OHLCVBar, User
    15	from app.schemas import OHLCVBarCreate, OHLCVBarResponse
    16	
    17	router = APIRouter(prefix="/instruments/{instrument_id}/ohlcv", tags=["market-data"])
    18	
    19	
    20	@router.post("", response_model=dict, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    21	def upload_ohlcv(
    22	    instrument_id: uuid.UUID,
    23	    bars: list[OHLCVBarCreate],
    24	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    25	    current_user: User = Depends(require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN")),
    26	):
    27	    """Batch upload OHLCV bars for an instrument. Uses upsert (INSERT ON CONFLICT UPDATE).
    28	    Platform admin only."""
    29	    instrument = db.query(Instrument).filter(Instrument.id == instrument_id).first()
    30	    if not instrument:
    31	        raise HTTPException(
    32	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Instrument not found"
    33	        )
    34	
    35	    if not bars:
    36	        return {"upserted": 0}
    37	
    38	    values = [
    39	        {
    40	            "instrument_id": instrument_id,
    41	            "timeframe": bar.timeframe,
    42	            "timestamp": bar.timestamp,
    43	            "open": bar.open,
    44	            "high": bar.high,
    45	            "low": bar.low,
    46	            "close": bar.close,
    47	            "volume": bar.volume,
    48	        }
    49	        for bar in bars
    50	    ]
    51	
    52	    stmt = insert(OHLCVBar).values(values)
    53	    stmt = stmt.on_conflict_do_update(
    54	        constraint="uq_ohlcv_bar",
    55	        set_={
    56	            "open": stmt.excluded.open,
    57	            "high": stmt.excluded.high,
    58	            "low": stmt.excluded.low,
    59	            "close": stmt.excluded.close,
    60	            "volume": stmt.excluded.volume,
    61	        },
    62	    )
    63	    db.execute(stmt)
    64	    db.commit()
    65	    return {"upserted": len(bars)}
    66	
    67	
    68	@router.get("", response_model=list[OHLCVBarResponse])
    69	def query_ohlcv(
    70	    instrument_id: uuid.UUID,
    71	    timeframe: str = Query(default="1d", max_length=5),
    72	    start: datetime | None = Query(default=None),
    73	    end: datetime | None = Query(default=None),
    74	    limit: int = Query(default=500, ge=1, le=5000),
    75	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    76	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    77	):
    78	    """Query OHLCV bars for an instrument. Any authenticated user can query."""
    79	    instrument = db.query(Instrument).filter(Instrument.id == instrument_id).first()
    80	    if not instrument:
    81	        raise HTTPException(
    82	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Instrument not found"
    83	        )
    84	
    85	    q = db.query(OHLCVBar).filter(
    86	        OHLCVBar.instrument_id == instrument_id,
    87	        OHLCVBar.timeframe == timeframe,
    88	    )
    89	    if start:
    90	        q = q.filter(OHLCVBar.timestamp >= start)
    91	    if end:
    92	        q = q.filter(OHLCVBar.timestamp <= end)
    93	
    94	    return q.order_by(OHLCVBar.timestamp).limit(limit).all()
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/strategies.py
     1	"""Strategy management endpoints (platform and tenant-owned)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import get_current_user
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import Strategy, Tenant, User
    14	from app.schemas import StrategyCreate, StrategyResponse, StrategyUpdate
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/strategies", tags=["strategies"])
    17	
    18	
    19	@router.get("", response_model=list[StrategyResponse])
    20	def list_strategies(
    21	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    22	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    23	):
    24	    """List strategies visible to the current user.
    25	    Platform strategies (owner_type=PLATFORM) are visible to all.
    26	    Tenant strategies are visible only to members of that tenant.
    27	    Platform admins see everything."""
    28	    if current_user.role == "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
    29	        return db.query(Strategy).order_by(Strategy.name).all()
    30	
    31	    return (
    32	        db.query(Strategy)
    33	        .filter(
    34	            (Strategy.owner_type == "PLATFORM")
    35	            | (Strategy.tenant_id == current_user.tenant_id)
    36	        )
    37	        .order_by(Strategy.name)
    38	        .all()
    39	    )
    40	
    41	
    42	@router.post("", response_model=StrategyResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    43	def create_strategy(
    44	    body: StrategyCreate,
    45	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    46	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    47	):
    48	    """Create a strategy.
    49	    - owner_type=PLATFORM, tenant_id=None: requires PLATFORM_ADMIN
    50	    - owner_type=TENANT, tenant_id set: requires TENANT_ADMIN or RESEARCHER in that tenant
    51	    """
    52	    if body.owner_type == "PLATFORM":
    53	        if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
    54	            raise HTTPException(
    55	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    56	                detail="Only platform admins can create platform strategies",
    57	            )
    58	        if body.tenant_id is not None:
    59	            raise HTTPException(
    60	                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    61	                detail="Platform strategies must not have a tenant_id",
    62	            )
    63	    elif body.owner_type == "TENANT":
    64	        if body.tenant_id is None:
    65	            raise HTTPException(
    66	                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    67	                detail="Tenant strategies must specify a tenant_id",
    68	            )
    69	        if current_user.role not in ("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "RESEARCHER"):
    70	            raise HTTPException(
    71	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    72	                detail="Insufficient permissions to create tenant strategies",
    73	            )
    74	        if (
    75	            current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN"
    76	            and current_user.tenant_id != body.tenant_id
    77	        ):
    78	            raise HTTPException(
    79	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    80	                detail="Access denied to this tenant",
    81	            )
    82	        tenant = db.query(Tenant).filter(Tenant.id == body.tenant_id).first()
    83	        if not tenant:
    84	            raise HTTPException(
    85	                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Tenant not found"
    86	            )
    87	    else:
    88	        raise HTTPException(
    89	            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    90	            detail="owner_type must be PLATFORM or TENANT",
    91	        )
    92	
    93	    strategy = Strategy(
    94	        tenant_id=body.tenant_id,
    95	        name=body.name,
    96	        description=body.description,
    97	        version=body.version,
    98	        owner_type=body.owner_type,
    99	        code_reference=body.code_reference,
   100	    )
   101	    db.add(strategy)
   102	    db.flush()
   103	
   104	    record_audit(
   105	        db,
   106	        action="strategy_created",
   107	        tenant_id=body.tenant_id,
   108	        user_id=current_user.id,
   109	        resource=f"strategy:{strategy.id}",
   110	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   111	    )
   112	    db.commit()
   113	    db.refresh(strategy)
   114	    return strategy
   115	
   116	
   117	@router.get("/{strategy_id}", response_model=StrategyResponse)
   118	def get_strategy(
   119	    strategy_id: uuid.UUID,
   120	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   121	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   122	):
   123	    """Get a single strategy. Checks visibility."""
   124	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == strategy_id).first()
   125	    if not strategy:
   126	        raise HTTPException(
   127	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
   128	        )
   129	
   130	    if strategy.owner_type == "TENANT" and current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   131	        if current_user.tenant_id != strategy.tenant_id:
   132	            raise HTTPException(
   133	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
   134	            )
   135	
   136	    return strategy
   137	
   138	
   139	@router.patch("/{strategy_id}", response_model=StrategyResponse)
   140	def update_strategy(
   141	    strategy_id: uuid.UUID,
   142	    body: StrategyUpdate,
   143	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   144	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   145	):
   146	    """Update a strategy. Ownership check enforced."""
   147	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == strategy_id).first()
   148	    if not strategy:
   149	        raise HTTPException(
   150	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
   151	        )
   152	
   153	    # Ownership check
   154	    if strategy.owner_type == "PLATFORM":
   155	        if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   156	            raise HTTPException(
   157	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
   158	                detail="Only platform admins can update platform strategies",
   159	            )
   160	    else:
   161	        if current_user.role not in ("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "RESEARCHER"):
   162	            raise HTTPException(
   163	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Insufficient permissions"
   164	            )
   165	        if (
   166	            current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN"
   167	            and current_user.tenant_id != strategy.tenant_id
   168	        ):
   169	            raise HTTPException(
   170	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
   171	            )
   172	
   173	    before = {
   174	        "name": strategy.name,
   175	        "version": strategy.version,
   176	        "status": strategy.status,
   177	    }
   178	    updates = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
   179	    for key, value in updates.items():
   180	        setattr(strategy, key, value)
   181	    db.flush()
   182	
   183	    record_audit(
   184	        db,
   185	        action="strategy_updated",
   186	        tenant_id=strategy.tenant_id,
   187	        user_id=current_user.id,
   188	        resource=f"strategy:{strategy_id}",
   189	        before_state=before,
   190	        after_state=updates,
   191	    )
   192	    db.commit()
   193	    db.refresh(strategy)
   194	    return strategy
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/strategy_configs.py
     1	"""Strategy configuration endpoints (tenant-scoped)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import Portfolio, Strategy, StrategyConfig, User
    14	from app.schemas import (
    15	    StrategyConfigCreate,
    16	    StrategyConfigResponse,
    17	    StrategyConfigUpdate,
    18	)
    19	
    20	router = APIRouter(
    21	    prefix="/tenants/{tenant_id}/strategy-configs", tags=["strategy-configs"]
    22	)
    23	
    24	
    25	def _check_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user: User) -> None:
    26	    """Raise 403 if user is not platform admin and does not belong to the tenant."""
    27	    if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN" and current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    28	        raise HTTPException(
    29	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
    30	        )
    31	
    32	
    33	@router.get("", response_model=list[StrategyConfigResponse])
    34	def list_strategy_configs(
    35	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    36	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    37	    current_user: User = Depends(
    38	        require_role(
    39	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    40	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
    41	            "TRADER",
    42	            "RESEARCHER",
    43	            "RISK_MANAGER",
    44	            "VIEWER",
    45	        )
    46	    ),
    47	):
    48	    """List strategy configs for a tenant."""
    49	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    50	    return (
    51	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
    52	        .filter(StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    53	        .order_by(StrategyConfig.created_at)
    54	        .all()
    55	    )
    56	
    57	
    58	@router.post(
    59	    "", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
    60	)
    61	def create_strategy_config(
    62	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    63	    body: StrategyConfigCreate,
    64	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    65	    current_user: User = Depends(
    66	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER")
    67	    ),
    68	):
    69	    """Create a strategy config within a tenant."""
    70	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    71	
    72	    # Validate portfolio belongs to this tenant
    73	    portfolio = (
    74	        db.query(Portfolio)
    75	        .filter(Portfolio.id == body.portfolio_id, Portfolio.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    76	        .first()
    77	    )
    78	    if not portfolio:
    79	        raise HTTPException(
    80	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    81	            detail="Portfolio not found in this tenant",
    82	        )
    83	
    84	    # Validate strategy exists and is accessible
    85	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == body.strategy_id).first()
    86	    if not strategy:
    87	        raise HTTPException(
    88	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
    89	        )
    90	    if strategy.owner_type == "TENANT" and strategy.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    91	        raise HTTPException(
    92	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    93	            detail="Strategy not accessible to this tenant",
    94	        )
    95	
    96	    config = StrategyConfig(
    97	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
    98	        portfolio_id=body.portfolio_id,
    99	        strategy_id=body.strategy_id,
   100	        parameters=body.parameters,
   101	        lifecycle_status=body.lifecycle_status,
   102	    )
   103	    db.add(config)
   104	    db.flush()
   105	
   106	    record_audit(
   107	        db,
   108	        action="strategy_config_created",
   109	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   110	        user_id=current_user.id,
   111	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config.id}",
   112	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   113	    )
   114	    db.commit()
   115	    db.refresh(config)
   116	    return config
   117	
   118	
   119	@router.get("/{config_id}", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse)
   120	def get_strategy_config(
   121	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   122	    config_id: uuid.UUID,
   123	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   124	    current_user: User = Depends(
   125	        require_role(
   126	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
   127	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
   128	            "TRADER",
   129	            "RESEARCHER",
   130	            "RISK_MANAGER",
   131	            "VIEWER",
   132	        )
   133	    ),
   134	):
   135	    """Get a single strategy config."""
   136	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   137	    config = (
   138	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
   139	        .filter(StrategyConfig.id == config_id, StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   140	        .first()
   141	    )
   142	    if not config:
   143	        raise HTTPException(
   144	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy config not found"
   145	        )
   146	    return config
   147	
   148	
   149	@router.patch("/{config_id}", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse)
   150	def update_strategy_config(
   151	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   152	    config_id: uuid.UUID,
   153	    body: StrategyConfigUpdate,
   154	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   155	    current_user: User = Depends(
   156	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER")
   157	    ),
   158	):
   159	    """Update a strategy config."""
   160	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   161	    config = (
   162	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
   163	        .filter(StrategyConfig.id == config_id, StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   164	        .first()
   165	    )
   166	    if not config:
   167	        raise HTTPException(
   168	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy config not found"
   169	        )
   170	
   171	    before = {
   172	        "parameters": config.parameters,
   173	        "lifecycle_status": config.lifecycle_status,
   174	        "is_active": config.is_active,
   175	    }
   176	    updates = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
   177	    for key, value in updates.items():
   178	        setattr(config, key, value)
   179	    db.flush()
   180	
   181	    record_audit(
   182	        db,
   183	        action="strategy_config_updated",
   184	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   185	        user_id=current_user.id,
   186	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config_id}",
   187	        before_state=before,
   188	        after_state=updates,
   189	    )
   190	    db.commit()
   191	    db.refresh(config)
   192	    return config
   193	
   194	
   195	@router.delete("/{config_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
   196	def delete_strategy_config(
   197	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   198	    config_id: uuid.UUID,
   199	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   200	    current_user: User = Depends(require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN")),
   201	):
   202	    """Delete a strategy config."""
   203	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   204	    config = (
   205	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
   206	        .filter(StrategyConfig.id == config_id, StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   207	        .first()
   208	    )
   209	    if not config:
   210	        raise HTTPException(
   211	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy config not found"
   212	        )
   213	
   214	    record_audit(
   215	        db,
   216	        action="strategy_config_deleted",
   217	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   218	        user_id=current_user.id,
   219	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config_id}",
   220	        before_state={
   221	            "parameters": config.parameters,
   222	            "lifecycle_status": config.lifecycle_status,
   223	            "is_active": config.is_active,
   224	        },
   225	    )
   226	    db.delete(config)
   227	    db.commit()
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/backtests.py
     1	"""Backtest run endpoints (tenant-scoped)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import BacktestRun, Portfolio, StrategyConfig, User
    14	from app.schemas import BacktestRunCreate, BacktestRunResponse
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests", tags=["backtests"])
    17	
    18	
    19	def _check_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user: User) -> None:
    20	    """Raise 403 if user is not platform admin and does not belong to the tenant."""
    21	    if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN" and current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    22	        raise HTTPException(
    23	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
    24	        )
    25	
    26	
    27	@router.get("", response_model=list[BacktestRunResponse])
    28	def list_backtests(
    29	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    30	    portfolio_id: uuid.UUID | None = Query(default=None),
    31	    backtest_status: str | None = Query(default=None, alias="status"),
    32	    limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=500),
    33	    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    34	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    35	    current_user: User = Depends(
    36	        require_role(
    37	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    38	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
    39	            "TRADER",
    40	            "RESEARCHER",
    41	            "RISK_MANAGER",
    42	            "VIEWER",
    43	        )
    44	    ),
    45	):
    46	    """List backtests for a tenant. Optional filters: portfolio_id, status."""
    47	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    48	
    49	    q = db.query(BacktestRun).filter(BacktestRun.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    50	    if portfolio_id:
    51	        q = q.filter(BacktestRun.portfolio_id == portfolio_id)
    52	    if backtest_status:
    53	        q = q.filter(BacktestRun.status == backtest_status)
    54	
    55	    return q.order_by(BacktestRun.created_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit).all()
    56	
    57	
    58	@router.post(
    59	    "", response_model=BacktestRunResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
    60	)
    61	def create_backtest(
    62	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    63	    body: BacktestRunCreate,
    64	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    65	    current_user: User = Depends(
    66	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER", "RESEARCHER")
    67	    ),
    68	):
    69	    """Create a new backtest run. Status starts as PENDING."""
    70	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    71	
    72	    # Validate portfolio belongs to this tenant
    73	    portfolio = (
    74	        db.query(Portfolio)
    75	        .filter(Portfolio.id == body.portfolio_id, Portfolio.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    76	        .first()
    77	    )
    78	    if not portfolio:
    79	        raise HTTPException(
    80	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    81	            detail="Portfolio not found in this tenant",
    82	        )
    83	
    84	    # Validate strategy config belongs to this tenant
    85	    config = (
    86	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
    87	        .filter(
    88	            StrategyConfig.id == body.strategy_config_id,
    89	            StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id,
    90	        )
    91	        .first()
    92	    )
    93	    if not config:
    94	        raise HTTPException(
    95	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    96	            detail="Strategy config not found in this tenant",
    97	        )
    98	
    99	    if body.start_date >= body.end_date:
   100	        raise HTTPException(
   101	            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
   102	            detail="start_date must be before end_date",
   103	        )
   104	
   105	    backtest = BacktestRun(
   106	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   107	        portfolio_id=body.portfolio_id,
   108	        strategy_config_id=body.strategy_config_id,
   109	        start_date=body.start_date,
   110	        end_date=body.end_date,
   111	        timeframe=body.timeframe,
   112	        parameters=body.parameters,
   113	    )
   114	    db.add(backtest)
   115	    db.flush()
   116	
   117	    record_audit(
   118	        db,
   119	        action="backtest_created",
   120	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   121	        user_id=current_user.id,
   122	        resource=f"backtest:{backtest.id}",
   123	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   124	    )
   125	    db.commit()
   126	    db.refresh(backtest)
   127	    return backtest
   128	
   129	
   130	@router.get("/{backtest_id}", response_model=BacktestRunResponse)
   131	def get_backtest(
   132	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   133	    backtest_id: uuid.UUID,
   134	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   135	    current_user: User = Depends(
   136	        require_role(
   137	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
   138	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
   139	            "TRADER",
   140	            "RESEARCHER",
   141	            "RISK_MANAGER",
   142	            "VIEWER",
   143	        )
   144	    ),
   145	):
   146	    """Get a single backtest run with metrics."""
   147	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   148	
   149	    backtest = (
   150	        db.query(BacktestRun)
   151	        .filter(BacktestRun.id == backtest_id, BacktestRun.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   152	        .first()
   153	    )
   154	    if not backtest:
   155	        raise HTTPException(
   156	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Backtest not found"
   157	        )
   158	    return backtest
### services/api-gateway/tests/conftest.py
     1	"""Shared pytest fixtures for the api-gateway test suite.
     2	
     3	The suite runs against a real PostgreSQL database (never the live one): the
     4	``DATABASE_URL`` environment variable is read *before* the application is
     5	imported so that ``app.database`` binds its engine to the test database.  The
     6	schema is dropped and recreated once per test session; each test function gets
     7	its own session while the tables (and committed rows) persist for the whole
     8	session.
     9	
    10	Typical usage::
    11	
    12	    def test_health(client):
    13	        assert client.get("/api/tenants").status_code == 401
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import os
    19	import sys
    20	import uuid
    21	from collections.abc import Generator
    22	from pathlib import Path
    23	
    24	# ``conftest.py`` lives at ``<service>/tests/conftest.py`` but the application
    25	# is imported as the top-level ``app`` package.  Make the service root
    26	# importable so the suite works no matter where pytest is invoked from.
    27	SERVICE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    28	if str(SERVICE_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    29	    sys.path.insert(0, str(SERVICE_ROOT))
    30	
    31	# Must be set before importing anything from ``app``.
    32	os.environ.setdefault("DATABASE_URL", "postgresql://aegis:aegis@localhost:5432/aegis")
    33	os.environ.setdefault("JWT_SECRET", "test-jwt-secret")
    34	
    35	import httpx  # noqa: E402
    36	import pytest  # noqa: E402
    37	from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402
    38	from sqlalchemy import create_engine  # noqa: E402
    39	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker  # noqa: E402
    40	
    41	from app.auth import create_access_token  # noqa: E402
    42	from app.config import settings  # noqa: E402
    43	from app.database import Base, get_db  # noqa: E402
    44	from main import app  # noqa: E402
    45	
    46	# Dedicated credentials for the platform admin bootstrapped by ``admin_token``.
    47	ADMIN_EMAIL = "admin@example.com"
    48	ADMIN_PASSWORD = "admin-test-pass"
    49	
    50	
    51	@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
    52	def engine():
    53	    """SQLAlchemy engine bound to the test database."""
    54	    engine = create_engine(settings.database_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    55	    try:
    56	        yield engine
    57	    finally:
    58	        engine.dispose()
    59	
    60	
    61	@pytest.fixture(scope="session", autouse=True)
    62	def _reset_database(engine):
    63	    """Drop and recreate every table once, giving the suite a clean slate."""
    64	    Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=engine)
    65	    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    66	    yield
    67	    # Best-effort cleanup; failures here must not fail the suite.
    68	    try:
    69	        Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=engine)
    70	    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - defensive teardown
    71	        pass
    72	
    73	
    74	@pytest.fixture
    75	def db_session(engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    76	    """A fresh ORM session for tests that need to inspect or seed the DB.
    77	
    78	    The session is intentionally *not* closed by the request override below, so
    79	    callers may keep using it after an API call; this fixture owns its lifetime.
    80	    """
    81	    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)()
    82	    try:
    83	        yield session
    84	    finally:
    85	        session.close()
    86	
    87	
    88	@pytest.fixture
    89	def client(engine) -> Generator[httpx.Client, None, None]:
    90	    """A synchronous httpx client wired to the FastAPI app.
    91	
    92	    ``TestClient`` is an ``httpx.Client`` subclass, so tests get the usual httpx
    93	    API.  ``get_db`` is overridden to hand every request a session bound to the
    94	    test engine instead of the application's configured database.
    95	    """
    96	
    97	    def override_get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    98	        db = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)()
    99	        try:
   100	            yield db
   101	        finally:
   102	            db.close()
   103	
   104	    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_get_db
   105	    # The session fixture prepares the schema; entering the client also runs the
   106	    # app lifespan (whose ``create_all`` is idempotent).
   107	    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
   108	        yield test_client
   109	    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
   110	
   111	
   112	def _ensure_platform_admin(engine) -> str:
   113	    """Return a platform-admin JWT, creating the admin on first use.
   114	
   115	    Prefers the real ``/api/auth/bootstrap`` endpoint so callers exercise the
   116	    bootstrap flow.  On later calls, when the platform is already bootstrapped,
   117	    the existing admin is looked up and a token is minted directly.
   118	    """
   119	    from app.models import User
   120	
   121	    session_factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)
   122	    with TestClient(app) as api:
   123	        response = api.post(
   124	            "/api/auth/bootstrap",
   125	            json={"email": ADMIN_EMAIL, "password": ADMIN_PASSWORD},
   126	        )
   127	        if response.status_code == 201:
   128	            return response.json()["access_token"]
   129	
   130	    assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
   131	    with session_factory() as db:
   132	        admin = db.query(User).filter_by(email=ADMIN_EMAIL).one()
   133	        return create_access_token(admin.id, admin.tenant_id, admin.role)
   134	
   135	
   136	@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
   137	def admin_token(engine) -> str:
   138	    """JWT for a bootstrapped platform admin (created once per session)."""
   139	    return _ensure_platform_admin(engine)
   140	
   141	
   142	@pytest.fixture
   143	def admin_headers(admin_token) -> dict[str, str]:
   144	    """Authorization header for the bootstrapped platform admin."""
   145	    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {admin_token}"}
   146	
   147	
   148	@pytest.fixture
   149	def tenant_with_admin(client, admin_headers) -> dict:
   150	    """Create a tenant plus its first TENANT_ADMIN and return auth details."""
   151	    suffix = uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]
   152	    tenant_name = f"Test Tenant {suffix}"
   153	    admin_email = f"tenant-admin-{suffix}@example.com"
   154	    admin_password = "tenant-admin-pass"
   155	
   156	    tenant_response = client.post(
   157	        "/api/tenants",
   158	        json={"name": tenant_name},
   159	        headers=admin_headers,
   160	    )
   161	    assert tenant_response.status_code == 201, tenant_response.text
   162	    tenant = tenant_response.json()
   163	
   164	    user_response = client.post(
   165	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant['id']}/users",
   166	        json={"email": admin_email, "password": admin_password, "role": "TENANT_ADMIN"},
   167	        headers=admin_headers,
   168	    )
   169	    assert user_response.status_code == 201, user_response.text
   170	    user = user_response.json()
   171	
   172	    login_response = client.post(
   173	        "/api/auth/login",
   174	        json={"email": admin_email, "password": admin_password},
   175	    )
   176	    assert login_response.status_code == 200, login_response.text
   177	    token = login_response.json()["access_token"]
   178	
   179	    return {
   180	        "tenant_id": tenant["id"],
   181	        "tenant": tenant,
   182	        "user_id": user["id"],
   183	        "user": user,
   184	        "email": admin_email,
   185	        "password": admin_password,
   186	        "token": token,
   187	        "headers": {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
   188	    }
### services/api-gateway/tests/test_phase2_api.py
     1	"""End-to-end tests for the Phase 2 API endpoints.
     2	
     3	Covers instrument CRUD, OHLCV batch upload/upsert/query, platform and tenant
     4	strategies, tenant-scoped strategy configs, and backtest runs.  All requests
     5	go through the ``/api`` prefix and use the shared fixtures from
     6	``conftest.py``.
     7	"""
     8	
     9	from __future__ import annotations
    10	
    11	import uuid
    12	from datetime import datetime, timedelta
    13	
    14	
    15	def _suffix() -> str:
    16	    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]
    17	
    18	
    19	def _create_instrument(
    20	    client, admin_headers, symbol: str | None = None, exchange: str = "NSE"
    21	) -> dict:
    22	    """Create an instrument as the platform admin and return the response body."""
    23	    body = {
    24	        "symbol": symbol or f"TST{_suffix()}",
    25	        "exchange": exchange,
    26	        "instrument_type": "EQUITY",
    27	        "name": f"Test Instrument {symbol or _suffix()}",
    28	        "lot_size": 1,
    29	        "tick_size": 0.05,
    30	    }
    31	    response = client.post("/api/instruments", json=body, headers=admin_headers)
    32	    assert response.status_code == 201, f"Instrument create failed: {response.text}"
    33	    return response.json()
    34	
    35	
    36	def _create_researcher(client, tenant_with_admin: dict) -> dict:
    37	    """Create a RESEARCHER user in the tenant and return login details."""
    38	    tenant_id = tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"]
    39	    suffix = _suffix()
    40	    email = f"researcher-{suffix}@example.com"
    41	    password = "researcher-pass"
    42	
    43	    create_response = client.post(
    44	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/users",
    45	        json={"email": email, "password": password, "role": "RESEARCHER"},
    46	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
    47	    )
    48	    assert (
    49	        create_response.status_code == 201
    50	    ), f"Researcher create failed: {create_response.text}"
    51	
    52	    login_response = client.post(
    53	        "/api/auth/login", json={"email": email, "password": password}
    54	    )
    55	    assert (
    56	        login_response.status_code == 200
    57	    ), f"Researcher login failed: {login_response.text}"
    58	    token = login_response.json()["access_token"]
    59	
    60	    return {
    61	        "user": create_response.json(),
    62	        "email": email,
    63	        "headers": {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
    64	    }
    65	
    66	
    67	def _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin: dict, name: str | None = None) -> dict:
    68	    """Create a portfolio in the tenant (as its admin) and return the response body."""
    69	    response = client.post(
    70	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/portfolios",
    71	        json={"name": name or f"Portfolio {_suffix()}", "starting_capital": 1_000_000},
    72	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
    73	    )
    74	    assert response.status_code == 201, f"Portfolio create failed: {response.text}"
    75	    return response.json()
    76	
    77	
    78	def _create_strategy_config(
    79	    client, tenant_with_admin: dict, strategy_id: uuid.UUID, portfolio_id: uuid.UUID
    80	) -> dict:
    81	    """Create a strategy config linking a strategy to a portfolio (as tenant admin)."""
    82	    response = client.post(
    83	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/strategy-configs",
    84	        json={
    85	            "portfolio_id": str(portfolio_id),
    86	            "strategy_id": str(strategy_id),
    87	            "parameters": {"lookback": 20},
    88	            "lifecycle_status": "DEVELOPMENT",
    89	        },
    90	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
    91	    )
    92	    assert (
    93	        response.status_code == 201
    94	    ), f"Strategy config create failed: {response.text}"
    95	    return response.json()
    96	
    97	
    98	def _create_backtest(
    99	    client, tenant_with_admin: dict, config: dict, portfolio_id: uuid.UUID
   100	) -> dict:
   101	    """Create a backtest run from an existing strategy config and return the body."""
   102	    response = client.post(
   103	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/backtests",
   104	        json={
   105	            "portfolio_id": str(portfolio_id),
   106	            "strategy_config_id": str(config["id"]),
   107	            "start_date": "2024-01-01",
   108	            "end_date": "2024-03-31",
   109	            "timeframe": "1d",
   110	            "parameters": {"capital": 100000},
   111	        },
   112	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   113	    )
   114	    assert response.status_code == 201, f"Backtest create failed: {response.text}"
   115	    return response.json()
   116

   138	    assert instrument["exchange"] == "NSE", "Created instrument exchange mismatch"

   140	    assert instrument["id"], "Instrument response should include an id"

   214	
   215	def test_ohlcv_upload_and_upserted_count(client, admin_headers):
   216	    """Platform admin batch-uploads OHLCV bars; the response reports the count."""
   217	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   218	    base = datetime(2024, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)
   219	    bars = [
   220	        {
   221	            "timeframe": "1d",
   222	            "timestamp": (base + timedelta(days=i)).isoformat(),
   223	            "open": 100.0 + i,
   224	            "high": 105.0 + i,
   225	            "low": 99.0 + i,
   226	            "close": 102.0 + i,
   227	            "volume": 1000 + i,
   228	        }
   229	        for i in range(5)
   230	    ]
   231	
   232	    response = client.post(
   233	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv", json=bars, headers=admin_headers
   234	    )
   235	    assert response.status_code == 201, f"OHLCV upload failed: {response.text}"
   236	    assert response.json() == {
   237	        "upserted": 5
   238	    }, f"Expected upserted=5, got {response.json()}"
   239	
   240	
   241	def test_ohlcv_upsert_updates_not_duplicates(client, admin_headers):
   242	    """Uploading the same timestamp twice updates the bar instead of duplicating."""
   243	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   244	    ts = datetime(2024, 2, 1, 9, 15, 0).isoformat()
   245	    bar_first = {
   246	        "timeframe": "1d",
   247	        "timestamp": ts,
   248	        "open": 50.0,
   249	        "high": 55.0,
   250	        "low": 49.0,
   251	        "close": 52.0,
   252	        "volume": 100,
   253	    }
   254	    bar_second = {
   255	        "timeframe": "1d",
   256	        "timestamp": ts,
   257	        "open": 50.0,
   258	        "high": 60.0,
   259	        "low": 49.5,
   260	        "close": 58.0,
   261	        "volume": 250,
   262	    }
   263	
   264	    first = client.post(
   265	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   266	        json=[bar_first],
   267	        headers=admin_headers,
   268	    )
   269	    assert first.status_code == 201, f"First upload failed: {first.text}"
   270	
   271	    second = client.post(
   272	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   273	        json=[bar_second],
   274	        headers=admin_headers,
   275	    )
   276	    assert second.status_code == 201, f"Second upload failed: {second.text}"
   277	    assert second.json() == {"upserted": 1}, "Re-upload should report 1 upserted bar"
   278	
   279	    query = client.get(
   280	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv", headers=admin_headers
   281	    )
   282	    assert query.status_code == 200, f"OHLCV query failed: {query.text}"
   283	    bars = query.json()
   284	    assert len(bars) == 1, f"Upsert should not duplicate bars, found {len(bars)}"
   285	    assert (
   286	        bars[0]["close"] == 58.0
   287	    ), f"Bar should hold updated close, got {bars[0]['close']}"
   288	    assert (
   289	        bars[0]["volume"] == 250
   290	    ), f"Bar should hold updated volume, got {bars[0]['volume']}"
   291	
   292	
   293	def test_ohlcv_query_timeframe_filter(client, admin_headers, tenant_with_admin):
   294	    """Querying bars returns only those matching the requested timeframe."""
   295	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   296	    ts = datetime(2024, 3, 1, 9, 15, 0).isoformat()
   297	    daily = {
   298	        "timeframe": "1d",
   299	        "timestamp": ts,
   300	        "open": 10.0,
   301	        "high": 11.0,
   302	        "low": 9.5,
   303	        "close": 10.5,
   304	        "volume": 500,
   305	    }
   306	    intraday = {
   307	        "timeframe": "1h",
   308	        "timestamp": ts,
   309	        "open": 10.0,
   310	        "high": 10.8,
   311	        "low": 9.8,
   312	        "close": 10.2,
   313	        "volume": 120,
   314	    }
   315	    upload = client.post(
   316	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   317	        json=[daily, intraday],
   318	        headers=admin_headers,
   319	    )
   320	    assert upload.status_code == 201, f"OHLCV upload failed: {upload.text}"
   321	
   322	    # Default query (timeframe=1d) returns only the daily bar.
   323	    response = client.get(
   324	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   325	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   326	    )
   327	    assert response.status_code == 200, f"OHLCV query failed: {response.text}"
   328	    bars = response.json()
   329	    assert len(bars) == 1, f"1d query should return exactly one bar, got {len(bars)}"
   330	    assert (
   331	        bars[0]["timeframe"] == "1d"
   332	    ), f"Wrong timeframe returned: {bars[0]['timeframe']}"
   333	    assert bars[0]["close"] == 10.5, "Returned bar data mismatch"
   334	
   335	    hourly = client.get(
   336	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv?timeframe=1h",
   337	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   338	    )
   339	    assert hourly.status_code == 200, f"1h query failed: {hourly.text}"
   340	    hourly_bars = hourly.json()
   341	    assert (
   342	        len(hourly_bars) == 1 and hourly_bars[0]["timeframe"] == "1h"
   343	    ), "1h query should return only the hourly bar"
   344	
   345	
   346	def test_ohlcv_query_date_range(client, admin_headers):
   347	    """Query with start/end filters returns only bars inside the range."""
   348	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   349	    base = datetime(2024, 5, 1, 0, 0, 0)
   350	    bars = [
   351	        {
   352	            "timeframe": "1d",
   353	            "timestamp": (base + timedelta(days=i)).isoformat(),
   354	            "open": 100.0,
   355	            "high": 101.0,
   356	            "low": 99.0,
   357	            "close": 100.5,
   358	            "volume": 10 + i,
   359	        }
   360	        for i in range(10)  # 2024-05-01 .. 2024-05-10
   361	    ]
   362	    upload = client.post(
   363	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv", json=bars, headers=admin_headers
   364	    )
   365	    assert upload.status_code == 201, f"OHLCV upload failed: {upload.text}"
   366	
   367	    params = {"start": "2024-05-03T00:00:00", "end": "2024-05-06T00:00:00"}
   368	    response = client.get(
   369	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   370	        params=params,
   371	        headers=admin_headers,
   372	    )
   373	    assert response.status_code == 200, f"Date-range query failed: {response.text}"
   374	    bars_in_range = response.json()
   375	    timestamps = [b["timestamp"] for b in bars_in_range]
   376	    assert (
   377	        len(bars_in_range) == 4
   378	    ), f"Expected 4 bars in range, got {len(bars_in_range)}: {timestamps}"
   379	    assert all(
   380	        "2024-05-03" <= t[:10] <= "2024-05-06" for t in timestamps
   381	    ), f"Bars outside the requested range were returned: {timestamps}"
   382

   434	def test_strategy_list_visibility(client, admin_headers, tenant_with_admin):
   435	    """Researchers see platform strategies + their own tenant's, not other tenants'."""
   436	    platform = client.post(
   437	        "/api/strategies",
   438	        json={
   439	            "name": f"Visible Platform Strategy {_suffix()}",
   440	            "owner_type": "PLATFORM",
   441	        },
   442	        headers=admin_headers,
   443	    )
   444	    assert (
   445	        platform.status_code == 201
   446	    ), f"Platform strategy create failed: {platform.text}"
   447	
   448	    own = client.post(
   449	        "/api/strategies",
   450	        json={
   451	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   452	            "name": f"Own Tenant Strategy {_suffix()}",
   453	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   454	        },
   455	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   456	    )
   457	    assert own.status_code == 201, f"Own tenant strategy create failed: {own.text}"
   458	
   459	    # Second tenant, to hold a strategy the researcher must NOT see.
   460	    other_tenant = client.post(
   461	        "/api/tenants",
   462	        json={"name": f"Other Tenant {_suffix()}"},
   463	        headers=admin_headers,
   464	    )
   465	    assert (
   466	        other_tenant.status_code == 201
   467	    ), f"Other tenant create failed: {other_tenant.text}"
   468	    foreign = client.post(
   469	        "/api/strategies",
   470	        json={
   471	            "tenant_id": other_tenant.json()["id"],
   472	            "name": f"Foreign Tenant Strategy {_suffix()}",
   473	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   474	        },
   475	        headers=admin_headers,
   476	    )
   477	    assert foreign.status_code == 201, f"Foreign strategy create failed: {foreign.text}"
   478	
   479	    researcher = _create_researcher(client, tenant_with_admin)
   480	    response = client.get("/api/strategies", headers=researcher["headers"])
   481	    assert response.status_code == 200, f"Strategy list failed: {response.text}"
   482	
   483	    strategies = response.json()
   484	    names = [s["name"] for s in strategies]
   485	    assert (
   486	        platform.json()["name"] in names
   487	    ), "Platform strategy should be visible to researchers"
   488	    assert own.json()["name"] in names, "Own tenant strategy should be visible"
   489	    assert (
   490	        foreign.json()["name"] not in names
   491	    ), "Another tenant's strategy must not be visible"
   492

   494	def test_strategy_update(client, admin_headers):
   495	    """Platform admin updates a platform strategy's version."""
   496	    created = client.post(
   497	        "/api/strategies",
   498	        json={
   499	            "name": f"Updatable Strategy {_suffix()}",
   500	            "version": "1.0.0",
   501	            "owner_type": "PLATFORM",
   502	        },
   503	        headers=admin_headers,
   504	    )
   505	    assert created.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {created.text}"
   506	    strategy_id = created.json()["id"]

   509	        f"/api/strategies/{strategy_id}",
   510	        json={"version": "2.0.0", "description": "Bumped to v2"},
   511	        headers=admin_headers,
   512	    )
   513	    assert response.status_code == 200, f"Strategy update failed: {response.text}"
   514	    updated = response.json()
   515	    assert (
   516	        updated["version"] == "2.0.0"
   517	    ), f"Version was not updated: {updated['version']}"
   518	    assert updated["description"] == "Bumped to v2", "Description was not updated"

   524	def test_strategy_config_create(client, admin_headers, tenant_with_admin):
   525	    """Create a config linking a strategy to a portfolio inside a tenant."""
   526	    portfolio = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   527	    strategy = client.post(
   528	        "/api/strategies",
   529	        json={
   530	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   531	            "name": f"Config Strategy {_suffix()}",
   532	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   533	        },
   534	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   535	    )
   536	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"
   537	
   538	    response = client.post(
   539	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/strategy-configs",
   540	        json={
   541	            "portfolio_id": portfolio["id"],
   542	            "strategy_id": strategy.json()["id"],
   543	            "parameters": {"lookback": 20, "threshold": 1.5},
   544	            "lifecycle_status": "DEVELOPMENT",
   545	        },
   546	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   547	    )
   548	    assert (
   549	        response.status_code == 201
   550	    ), f"Strategy config create failed: {response.text}"
   551	    config = response.json()
   552	    assert (
   553	        config["portfolio_id"] == portfolio["id"]
   554	    ), "Config should reference the portfolio"
   555	    assert (
   556	        config["strategy_id"] == strategy.json()["id"]
   557	    ), "Config should reference the strategy"
   558	    assert (
   559	        config["tenant_id"] == tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"]
   560	    ), "Config should be tenant-scoped"
   561	    assert (
   562	        config["lifecycle_status"] == "DEVELOPMENT"
   563	    ), "Default lifecycle status mismatch"
   564	    assert config["is_active"] is True, "New config should be active"
   565

   601	def test_strategy_config_delete(client, tenant_with_admin):
   602	    """Delete returns 204; a subsequent get returns 404."""
   603	    portfolio = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   604	    strategy = client.post(
   605	        "/api/strategies",
   606	        json={
   607	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   608	            "name": f"Deletable Config Strategy {_suffix()}",
   609	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   610	        },

   612	    )
   613	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"

   615	        client, tenant_with_admin, strategy.json()["id"], portfolio["id"]
   616	    )

   619	    headers = tenant_with_admin["headers"]
   620	    delete_response = client.delete(

   622	    )
   623	    assert (
   624	        delete_response.status_code == 204
   625	    ), f"Delete should return 204, got {delete_response.status_code}: {delete_response.text}"
   626

   628	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/strategy-configs/{config['id']}", headers=headers
   629	    )
   630	    assert (
   631	        get_response.status_code == 404
   632	    ), f"Deleted config should 404, got {get_response.status_code}"

   634	
   635	def test_strategy_config_cross_tenant_validation(
   636	    client, admin_headers, tenant_with_admin
   637	):
   638	    """A config cannot reference another tenant's portfolio or tenant strategy."""
   639	    # First tenant (from the fixture) owns the portfolio and tenant strategy.
   640	    portfolio = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   641	    strategy = client.post(
   642	        "/api/strategies",
   643	        json={
   644	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   645	            "name": f"Cross Tenant Strategy {_suffix()}",
   646	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   647	        },
   648	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   649	    )
   650	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"
   651	
   652	    # Second tenant, whose admin attempts to use the first tenant's resources.
   653	    other_tenant = client.post(
   654	        "/api/tenants",
   655	        json={"name": f"Cross Tenant {_suffix()}"},
   656	        headers=admin_headers,
   657	    )
   658	    assert (
   659	        other_tenant.status_code == 201
   660	    ), f"Other tenant create failed: {other_tenant.text}"
   661	    other_tenant_id = other_tenant.json()["id"]
   662	
   663	    other_admin_email = f"other-admin-{_suffix()}@example.com"
   664	    user_response = client.post(
   665	        f"/api/tenants/{other_tenant_id}/users",
   666	        json={
   667	            "email": other_admin_email,
   668	            "password": "other-admin-pass",
   669	            "role": "TENANT_ADMIN",
   670	        },
   671	        headers=admin_headers,
   672	    )
   673	    assert (
   674	        user_response.status_code == 201
   675	    ), f"Other tenant admin create failed: {user_response.text}"
   676	    login_response = client.post(
   677	        "/api/auth/login",
   678	        json={"email": other_admin_email, "password": "other-admin-pass"},
   679	    )
   680	    assert (
   681	        login_response.status_code == 200
   682	    ), f"Other tenant admin login failed: {login_response.text}"
   683	    other_headers = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {login_response.json()['access_token']}"}
   684	
   685	    # Foreign portfolio rejected.
   686	    portfolio_attempt = client.post(
   687	        f"/api/tenants/{other_tenant_id}/strategy-configs",
   688	        json={"portfolio_id": portfolio["id"], "strategy_id": strategy.json()["id"]},
   689	        headers=other_headers,
   690	    )
   691	    assert (
   692	        portfolio_attempt.status_code == 404
   693	    ), f"Foreign portfolio should be rejected (404), got {portfolio_attempt.status_code}: {portfolio_attempt.text}"
   694	
   695	    # Foreign tenant strategy rejected.
   696	    own_other_portfolio = _create_portfolio(
   697	        client, {"tenant_id": other_tenant_id, "headers": other_headers}
   698	    )
   699	    strategy_attempt = client.post(
   700	        f"/api/tenants/{other_tenant_id}/strategy-configs",
   701	        json={
   702	            "portfolio_id": own_other_portfolio["id"],
   703	            "strategy_id": strategy.json()["id"],
   704	        },
   705	        headers=other_headers,
   706	    )
   707	    assert (
   708	        strategy_attempt.status_code == 403
   709	    ), f"Foreign tenant strategy should be rejected (403), got {strategy_attempt.status_code}: {strategy_attempt.text}"
   710	
   711

   715	def test_backtest_create(client, tenant_with_admin):
   716	    """A researcher creates a backtest run; it starts as PENDING."""
   717	    portfolio = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   718	    strategy = client.post(
   719	        "/api/strategies",
   720	        json={
   721	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   722	            "name": f"Backtest Strategy {_suffix()}",
   723	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   724	        },
   725	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   726	    )
   727	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"
   728	    config = _create_strategy_config(
   729	        client, tenant_with_admin, strategy.json()["id"], portfolio["id"]
   730	    )
   731	
   732	    researcher = _create_researcher(client, tenant_with_admin)
   733	    response = client.post(
   734	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/backtests",
   735	        json={
   736	            "portfolio_id": portfolio["id"],
   737	            "strategy_config_id": config["id"],
   738	            "start_date": "2024-01-01",
   739	            "end_date": "2024-06-30",
   740	            "timeframe": "1d",
   741	        },
   742	        headers=researcher["headers"],
   743	    )
   744	    assert response.status_code == 201, f"Backtest create failed: {response.text}"
   745	    backtest = response.json()
   746	    assert (
   747	        backtest["status"] == "PENDING"
   748	    ), f"New backtest should be PENDING, got {backtest['status']}"
   749	    assert backtest["start_date"] == "2024-01-01", "start_date mismatch"
   750	    assert backtest["end_date"] == "2024-06-30", "end_date mismatch"
   751	    assert backtest["metrics"] is None, "New backtest should have no metrics yet"
   752

   754	def test_backtest_invalid_date_range(client, tenant_with_admin):
   755	    """start_date >= end_date returns 400."""
   756	    portfolio = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   757	    strategy = client.post(
   758	        "/api/strategies",
   759	        json={
   760	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   761	            "name": f"Invalid Dates Strategy {_suffix()}",
   762	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   763	        },
   764	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   765	    )
   766	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"
   767	    config = _create_strategy_config(
   768	        client, tenant_with_admin, strategy.json()["id"], portfolio["id"]
   769	    )
   770	
   771	    tenant_id = tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"]
   772	    headers = tenant_with_admin["headers"]
   773	
   774	    # start == end
   775	    equal_dates = client.post(
   776	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests",
   777	        json={
   778	            "portfolio_id": portfolio["id"],
   779	            "strategy_config_id": config["id"],
   780	            "start_date": "2024-03-01",
   781	            "end_date": "2024-03-01",
   782	        },
   783	        headers=headers,
   784	    )
   785	    assert (
   786	        equal_dates.status_code == 400
   787	    ), f"start_date == end_date should return 400, got {equal_dates.status_code}: {equal_dates.text}"
   788	
   789	    # start > end
   790	    reversed_dates = client.post(
   791	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests",
   792	        json={
   793	            "portfolio_id": portfolio["id"],
   794	            "strategy_config_id": config["id"],
   795	            "start_date": "2024-06-30",
   796	            "end_date": "2024-01-01",
   797	        },
   798	        headers=headers,
   799	    )
   800	    assert (
   801	        reversed_dates.status_code == 400
   802	    ), f"start_date > end_date should return 400, got {reversed_dates.status_code}: {reversed_dates.text}"
   803	
   804	
   805	def test_backtest_list_and_get(client, tenant_with_admin):
   806	    """List backtests (with optional filters) and fetch one by ID."""
   807	    portfolio_a = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   808	    portfolio_b = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   809	    strategy = client.post(
   810	        "/api/strategies",
   811	        json={
   812	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   813	            "name": f"List Strategy {_suffix()}",
   814	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   815	        },
   816	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   817	    )
   818	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"
   819	    strategy_id = strategy.json()["id"]
   820	    config_a = _create_strategy_config(
   821	        client, tenant_with_admin, strategy_id, portfolio_a["id"]
   822	    )
   823	    config_b = _create_strategy_config(
   824	        client, tenant_with_admin, strategy_id, portfolio_b["id"]
   825	    )
   826	    backtest_a = _create_backtest(
   827	        client, tenant_with_admin, config_a, portfolio_a["id"]
   828	    )
   829	    backtest_b = _create_backtest(
   830	        client, tenant_with_admin, config_b, portfolio_b["id"]
   831	    )
   832	
   833	    tenant_id = tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"]
   834	    headers = tenant_with_admin["headers"]
   835	
   836	    # Plain list contains both.
   837	    list_response = client.get(f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests", headers=headers)
   838	    assert (
   839	        list_response.status_code == 200
   840	    ), f"Backtest list failed: {list_response.text}"
   841	    listed_ids = [b["id"] for b in list_response.json()]
   842	    assert backtest_a["id"] in listed_ids, "Backtest A missing from list"
   843	    assert backtest_b["id"] in listed_ids, "Backtest B missing from list"
   844	
   845	    # Filter by portfolio_id.
   846	    filtered = client.get(
   847	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests",
   848	        params={"portfolio_id": portfolio_a["id"]},
   849	        headers=headers,
   850	    )
   851	    assert (
   852	        filtered.status_code == 200
   853	    ), f"Filtered backtest list failed: {filtered.text}"
   854	    filtered_ids = [b["id"] for b in filtered.json()]
   855	    assert filtered_ids == [
   856	        backtest_a["id"]
   857	    ], f"portfolio_id filter should return only backtest A: {filtered_ids}"
   858	
   859	    # Filter by status.
   860	    by_status = client.get(
   861	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests",
   862	        params={"status": "PENDING"},
   863	        headers=headers,
   864	    )
   865	    assert (
   866	        by_status.status_code == 200
   867	    ), f"Status-filtered list failed: {by_status.text}"
   868	    status_ids = [b["id"] for b in by_status.json()]
   869	    assert (
   870	        backtest_a["id"] in status_ids and backtest_b["id"] in status_ids
   871	    ), "Both PENDING backtests should be listed"
   872	
   873	    # Get single.
   874	    get_response = client.get(
   875	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests/{backtest_a['id']}", headers=headers
   876	    )
   877	    assert get_response.status_code == 200, f"Backtest get failed: {get_response.text}"
   878	    assert get_response.json()["id"] == backtest_a["id"], "Fetched backtest id mismatch"
   879	
   880	    # Unknown id -> 404.
   881	    missing = client.get(
   882	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests/{uuid.uuid4()}", headers=headers
   883	    )
   884	    assert (
   885	        missing.status_code == 404
   886	    ), f"Unknown backtest should 404, got {missing.status_code}"
   887	
   888	
   889	def test_backtest_tenant_isolation(client, admin_headers, tenant_with_admin):
   890	    """A user from another tenant cannot see (or create in) another tenant's backtests."""
   891	    portfolio = _create_portfolio(client, tenant_with_admin)
   892	    strategy = client.post(
   893	        "/api/strategies",
   894	        json={
   895	            "tenant_id": tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"],
   896	            "name": f"Isolated Strategy {_suffix()}",
   897	            "owner_type": "TENANT",
   898	        },
   899	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   900	    )
   901	    assert strategy.status_code == 201, f"Strategy create failed: {strategy.text}"
   902	    config = _create_strategy_config(
   903	        client, tenant_with_admin, strategy.json()["id"], portfolio["id"]
   904	    )
   905	    backtest = _create_backtest(client, tenant_with_admin, config, portfolio["id"])
   906	
   907	    # Foreign tenant + RESEARCHER user.
   908	    other_tenant = client.post(
   909	        "/api/tenants",
   910	        json={"name": f"Isolation Tenant {_suffix()}"},
   911	        headers=admin_headers,
   912	    )
   913	    assert (
   914	        other_tenant.status_code == 201
   915	    ), f"Other tenant create failed: {other_tenant.text}"
   916	    other_tenant_id = other_tenant.json()["id"]
   917	    researcher_email = f"foreign-researcher-{_suffix()}@example.com"
   918	    user_response = client.post(
   919	        f"/api/tenants/{other_tenant_id}/users",
   920	        json={
   921	            "email": researcher_email,
   922	            "password": "foreign-pass-123",
   923	            "role": "RESEARCHER",
   924	        },
   925	        headers=admin_headers,
   926	    )
   927	    assert (
   928	        user_response.status_code == 201
   929	    ), f"Foreign researcher create failed: {user_response.text}"
   930	    login_response = client.post(
   931	        "/api/auth/login",
   932	        json={"email": researcher_email, "password": "foreign-pass-123"},
   933	    )
   934	    assert (
   935	        login_response.status_code == 200
   936	    ), f"Foreign researcher login failed: {login_response.text}"
   937	    foreign_headers = {
   938	        "Authorization": f"Bearer {login_response.json()['access_token']}"
   939	    }
   940	
   941	    # List is tenant-scoped: 403 for a foreign tenant id.
   942	    list_response = client.get(
   943	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/backtests",
   944	        headers=foreign_headers,
   945	    )
   946	    assert (
   947	        list_response.status_code == 403
   948	    ), f"Foreign user listing another tenant's backtests should get 403, got {list_response.status_code}: {list_response.text}"
   949	
   950	    # Get is also tenant-scoped.
   951	    get_response = client.get(
   952	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/backtests/{backtest['id']}",
   953	        headers=foreign_headers,
   954	    )
   955	    assert (
   956	        get_response.status_code == 403
   957	    ), f"Foreign user fetching another tenant's backtest should get 403, got {get_response.status_code}: {get_response.text}"
   958	
   959	    # Creating in a foreign tenant is rejected too.
   960	    create_response = client.post(
   961	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_with_admin['tenant_id']}/backtests",
   962	        json={
   963	            "portfolio_id": portfolio["id"],
   964	            "strategy_config_id": config["id"],
   965	            "start_date": "2024-01-01",
   966	            "end_date": "2024-02-01",
   967	        },
   968	        headers=foreign_headers,
   969	    )
   970	    assert (
   971	        create_response.status_code == 403
   972	    ), f"Foreign user creating in another tenant should get 403, got {create_response.status_code}: {create_response.text}"

     1	name: CI

     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]

     7	    branches: [main]
     8	
     9	permissions:
    10	  contents: read
    11	
    12	jobs:
    13	  lint:
    14	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    15	    steps:
    16	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    17	
    18	      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
    19	        with:
    20	          python-version: "3.12"
    21	          cache: pip
    22	
    23	      - name: Install ruff
    24	        run: pip install ruff
    25	
    26	      - name: Run ruff check
    27	        run: ruff check services/api-gateway/
    28	
    29	      - name: Run ruff format check
    30	        run: ruff format --check services/api-gateway/
    31	
    32	  test:
    33	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    34	    env:
    35	      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://aegis:testpass@localhost:5432/aegis_test
    36	      JWT_SECRET: test-jwt-secret
    37	    services:
    38	      postgres:
    39	        image: postgres:18-alpine
    40	        env:
    41	          POSTGRES_DB: aegis_test
    42	          POSTGRES_USER: aegis
    43	          POSTGRES_PASSWORD: testpass
    44	        ports:
    45	          - 5432:5432
    46	        options: >-
    47	          --health-cmd "pg_isready -U aegis -d aegis_test"
    48	          --health-interval 10s
    49	          --health-timeout 5s
    50	          --health-retries 5
    51	    steps:
    52	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    53	
    54	      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
    55	        with:
    56	          python-version: "3.12"
    57	          cache: pip
    58	
    59	      - name: Install dependencies
    60	        run: |
    61	          pip install -r services/api-gateway/requirements.txt
    62	          pip install -r services/api-gateway/requirements-test.txt
    63	
    64	      - name: Run tests
    65	        run: pytest services/api-gateway/tests/ -v
    66	
    67	  build:
    68	    needs: [lint, test]
    69	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    70	    permissions:
    71	      contents: read
    72	      packages: write
    73	    steps:
    74	      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
    75	
    76	      - name: Set up Docker Buildx
    77	        uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v3
    78	
    79	      - name: Log in to GitHub Container Registry
    80	        if: github.event_name == 'push'
    81	        uses: docker/login-action@v3
    82	        with:
    83	          registry: ghcr.io
    84	          username: ${{ github.actor }}
    85	          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    86	
    87	      - name: Extract Docker metadata
    88	        id: meta
    89	        uses: docker/metadata-action@v5
    90	        with:
    91	          images: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository }}/api-gateway
    92	          tags: |
    93	            type=sha
    94	            type=raw,value=latest,enable={{is_default_branch}}
    95	
    96	      - name: Build and push
    97	        uses: docker/build-push-action@v6
    98	        with:
    99	          context: services/api-gateway
   100	          push: ${{ github.event_name == 'push' }}
   101	          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
   102	          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}
   103	          cache-from: type=gha
   104	          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
