### services/api-gateway/app/routers/instruments.py
     1	"""Instrument management endpoints."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import get_current_user, require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import Instrument, User
    14	from app.schemas import InstrumentCreate, InstrumentResponse
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/instruments", tags=["instruments"])
    17	
    18	
    19	@router.get("", response_model=list[InstrumentResponse])
    20	def list_instruments(
    21	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    22	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    23	):
    24	    """List all active instruments. Any authenticated user can view."""
    25	    return (
    26	        db.query(Instrument)
    27	        .filter(Instrument.is_active.is_(True))
    28	        .order_by(Instrument.symbol)
    29	        .all()
    30	    )
    31	
    32	
    33	@router.post("", response_model=InstrumentResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    34	def create_instrument(
    35	    body: InstrumentCreate,
    36	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    37	    current_user: User = Depends(require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN")),
    38	):
    39	    """Create a new instrument. Platform admin only."""
    40	    existing = (
    41	        db.query(Instrument)
    42	        .filter(Instrument.symbol == body.symbol, Instrument.exchange == body.exchange)
    43	        .first()
    44	    )
    45	    if existing:
    46	        raise HTTPException(
    47	            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
    48	            detail=f"Instrument {body.symbol}:{body.exchange} already exists",
    49	        )
    50	
    51	    instrument = Instrument(
    52	        symbol=body.symbol,
    53	        exchange=body.exchange,
    54	        instrument_type=body.instrument_type,
    55	        name=body.name,
    56	        lot_size=body.lot_size,
    57	        tick_size=body.tick_size,
    58	    )
    59	    db.add(instrument)
    60	    db.flush()
    61	
    62	    record_audit(
    63	        db,
    64	        action="instrument_created",
    65	        user_id=current_user.id,
    66	        resource=f"instrument:{instrument.id}",
    67	        after_state=body.model_dump(),
    68	    )
    69	    db.commit()
    70	    db.refresh(instrument)
    71	    return instrument
    72	
    73	
    74	@router.get("/{instrument_id}", response_model=InstrumentResponse)
    75	def get_instrument(
    76	    instrument_id: uuid.UUID,
    77	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    78	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    79	):
    80	    """Get a single instrument by ID. Any authenticated user can view."""
    81	    instrument = db.query(Instrument).filter(Instrument.id == instrument_id).first()
    82	    if not instrument:
    83	        raise HTTPException(
    84	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Instrument not found"
    85	        )
    86	    return instrument
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/ohlcv.py
     1	"""OHLCV market data endpoints (nested under instruments)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	from datetime import datetime
     7	
     8	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
     9	from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert
    10	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    11	
    12	from app.auth import get_current_user, require_role
    13	from app.database import get_db
    14	from app.models import Instrument, OHLCVBar, User
    15	from app.schemas import OHLCVBarCreate, OHLCVBarResponse
    16	
    17	router = APIRouter(prefix="/instruments/{instrument_id}/ohlcv", tags=["market-data"])
    18	
    19	
    20	@router.post("", response_model=dict, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    21	def upload_ohlcv(
    22	    instrument_id: uuid.UUID,
    23	    bars: list[OHLCVBarCreate],
    24	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    25	    current_user: User = Depends(require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN")),
    26	):
    27	    """Batch upload OHLCV bars for an instrument. Uses upsert (INSERT ON CONFLICT UPDATE).
    28	    Platform admin only."""
    29	    instrument = db.query(Instrument).filter(Instrument.id == instrument_id).first()
    30	    if not instrument:
    31	        raise HTTPException(
    32	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Instrument not found"
    33	        )
    34	
    35	    if not bars:
    36	        return {"upserted": 0}
    37	
    38	    values = [
    39	        {
    40	            "instrument_id": instrument_id,
    41	            "timeframe": bar.timeframe,
    42	            "timestamp": bar.timestamp,
    43	            "open": bar.open,
    44	            "high": bar.high,
    45	            "low": bar.low,
    46	            "close": bar.close,
    47	            "volume": bar.volume,
    48	        }
    49	        for bar in bars
    50	    ]
    51	
    52	    stmt = insert(OHLCVBar).values(values)
    53	    stmt = stmt.on_conflict_do_update(
    54	        constraint="uq_ohlcv_bar",
    55	        set_={
    56	            "open": stmt.excluded.open,
    57	            "high": stmt.excluded.high,
    58	            "low": stmt.excluded.low,
    59	            "close": stmt.excluded.close,
    60	            "volume": stmt.excluded.volume,
    61	        },
    62	    )
    63	    db.execute(stmt)
    64	    db.commit()
    65	    return {"upserted": len(bars)}
    66	
    67	
    68	@router.get("", response_model=list[OHLCVBarResponse])
    69	def query_ohlcv(
    70	    instrument_id: uuid.UUID,
    71	    timeframe: str = Query(default="1d", max_length=5),
    72	    start: datetime | None = Query(default=None),
    73	    end: datetime | None = Query(default=None),
    74	    limit: int = Query(default=500, ge=1, le=5000),
    75	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    76	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    77	):
    78	    """Query OHLCV bars for an instrument. Any authenticated user can query."""
    79	    instrument = db.query(Instrument).filter(Instrument.id == instrument_id).first()
    80	    if not instrument:
    81	        raise HTTPException(
    82	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Instrument not found"
    83	        )
    84	
    85	    q = db.query(OHLCVBar).filter(
    86	        OHLCVBar.instrument_id == instrument_id,
    87	        OHLCVBar.timeframe == timeframe,
    88	    )
    89	    if start:
    90	        q = q.filter(OHLCVBar.timestamp >= start)
    91	    if end:
    92	        q = q.filter(OHLCVBar.timestamp <= end)
    93	
    94	    return q.order_by(OHLCVBar.timestamp).limit(limit).all()
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/strategies.py
     1	"""Strategy management endpoints (platform and tenant-owned)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import get_current_user
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import Strategy, Tenant, User
    14	from app.schemas import StrategyCreate, StrategyResponse, StrategyUpdate
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/strategies", tags=["strategies"])
    17	
    18	
    19	@router.get("", response_model=list[StrategyResponse])
    20	def list_strategies(
    21	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    22	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    23	):
    24	    """List strategies visible to the current user.
    25	    Platform strategies (owner_type=PLATFORM) are visible to all.
    26	    Tenant strategies are visible only to members of that tenant.
    27	    Platform admins see everything."""
    28	    if current_user.role == "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
    29	        return db.query(Strategy).order_by(Strategy.name).all()
    30	
    31	    return (
    32	        db.query(Strategy)
    33	        .filter(
    34	            (Strategy.owner_type == "PLATFORM")
    35	            | (Strategy.tenant_id == current_user.tenant_id)
    36	        )
    37	        .order_by(Strategy.name)
    38	        .all()
    39	    )
    40	
    41	
    42	@router.post("", response_model=StrategyResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
    43	def create_strategy(
    44	    body: StrategyCreate,
    45	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    46	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    47	):
    48	    """Create a strategy.
    49	    - owner_type=PLATFORM, tenant_id=None: requires PLATFORM_ADMIN
    50	    - owner_type=TENANT, tenant_id set: requires TENANT_ADMIN or RESEARCHER in that tenant
    51	    """
    52	    if body.owner_type == "PLATFORM":
    53	        if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
    54	            raise HTTPException(
    55	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    56	                detail="Only platform admins can create platform strategies",
    57	            )
    58	        if body.tenant_id is not None:
    59	            raise HTTPException(
    60	                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    61	                detail="Platform strategies must not have a tenant_id",
    62	            )
    63	    elif body.owner_type == "TENANT":
    64	        if body.tenant_id is None:
    65	            raise HTTPException(
    66	                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    67	                detail="Tenant strategies must specify a tenant_id",
    68	            )
    69	        if current_user.role not in ("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "RESEARCHER"):
    70	            raise HTTPException(
    71	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    72	                detail="Insufficient permissions to create tenant strategies",
    73	            )
    74	        if (
    75	            current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN"
    76	            and current_user.tenant_id != body.tenant_id
    77	        ):
    78	            raise HTTPException(
    79	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    80	                detail="Access denied to this tenant",
    81	            )
    82	        tenant = db.query(Tenant).filter(Tenant.id == body.tenant_id).first()
    83	        if not tenant:
    84	            raise HTTPException(
    85	                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Tenant not found"
    86	            )
    87	    else:
    88	        raise HTTPException(
    89	            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
    90	            detail="owner_type must be PLATFORM or TENANT",
    91	        )
    92	
    93	    strategy = Strategy(
    94	        tenant_id=body.tenant_id,
    95	        name=body.name,
    96	        description=body.description,
    97	        version=body.version,
    98	        owner_type=body.owner_type,
    99	        code_reference=body.code_reference,
   100	    )
   101	    db.add(strategy)
   102	    db.flush()
   103	
   104	    record_audit(
   105	        db,
   106	        action="strategy_created",
   107	        tenant_id=body.tenant_id,
   108	        user_id=current_user.id,
   109	        resource=f"strategy:{strategy.id}",
   110	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   111	    )
   112	    db.commit()
   113	    db.refresh(strategy)
   114	    return strategy
   115	
   116	
   117	@router.get("/{strategy_id}", response_model=StrategyResponse)
   118	def get_strategy(
   119	    strategy_id: uuid.UUID,
   120	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   121	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   122	):
   123	    """Get a single strategy. Checks visibility."""
   124	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == strategy_id).first()
   125	    if not strategy:
   126	        raise HTTPException(
   127	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
   128	        )
   129	
   130	    if strategy.owner_type == "TENANT" and current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   131	        if current_user.tenant_id != strategy.tenant_id:
   132	            raise HTTPException(
   133	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
   134	            )
   135	
   136	    return strategy
   137	
   138	
   139	@router.patch("/{strategy_id}", response_model=StrategyResponse)
   140	def update_strategy(
   141	    strategy_id: uuid.UUID,
   142	    body: StrategyUpdate,
   143	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   144	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   145	):
   146	    """Update a strategy. Ownership check enforced."""
   147	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == strategy_id).first()
   148	    if not strategy:
   149	        raise HTTPException(
   150	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
   151	        )
   152	
   153	    # Ownership check
   154	    if strategy.owner_type == "PLATFORM":
   155	        if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   156	            raise HTTPException(
   157	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
   158	                detail="Only platform admins can update platform strategies",
   159	            )
   160	    else:
   161	        if current_user.role not in ("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "RESEARCHER"):
   162	            raise HTTPException(
   163	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Insufficient permissions"
   164	            )
   165	        if (
   166	            current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN"
   167	            and current_user.tenant_id != strategy.tenant_id
   168	        ):
   169	            raise HTTPException(
   170	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
   171	            )
   172	
   173	    before = {
   174	        "name": strategy.name,
   175	        "version": strategy.version,
   176	        "status": strategy.status,
   177	    }
   178	    updates = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
   179	    for key, value in updates.items():
   180	        setattr(strategy, key, value)
   181	    db.flush()
   182	
   183	    record_audit(
   184	        db,
   185	        action="strategy_updated",
   186	        tenant_id=strategy.tenant_id,
   187	        user_id=current_user.id,
   188	        resource=f"strategy:{strategy_id}",
   189	        before_state=before,
   190	        after_state=updates,
   191	    )
   192	    db.commit()
   193	    db.refresh(strategy)
   194	    return strategy
### services/api-gateway/app/routers/strategy_configs.py
     1	"""Strategy configuration endpoints (tenant-scoped)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import Portfolio, Strategy, StrategyConfig, User
    14	from app.schemas import (
    15	    StrategyConfigCreate,

    18	)
    19	
    20	router = APIRouter(
    21	    prefix="/tenants/{tenant_id}/strategy-configs", tags=["strategy-configs"]
    22	)
    23	
    24	
    25	def _check_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user: User) -> None:
    26	    """Raise 403 if user is not platform admin and does not belong to the tenant."""
    27	    if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN" and current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    28	        raise HTTPException(
    29	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
    30	        )

    33	@router.get("", response_model=list[StrategyConfigResponse])
    34	def list_strategy_configs(
    35	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    36	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    37	    current_user: User = Depends(
    38	        require_role(
    39	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    40	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
    41	            "TRADER",
    42	            "RESEARCHER",
    43	            "RISK_MANAGER",
    44	            "VIEWER",
    45	        )
    46	    ),
    47	):
    48	    """List strategy configs for a tenant."""
    49	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    50	    return (
    51	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
    52	        .filter(StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    53	        .order_by(StrategyConfig.created_at)
    54	        .all()
    55	    )

    58	@router.post(
    59	    "", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
    60	)
    61	def create_strategy_config(
    62	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    63	    body: StrategyConfigCreate,
    64	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    65	    current_user: User = Depends(
    66	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER")
    67	    ),
    68	):
    69	    """Create a strategy config within a tenant."""
    70	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    71	
    72	    # Validate portfolio belongs to this tenant
    73	    portfolio = (
    74	        db.query(Portfolio)
    75	        .filter(Portfolio.id == body.portfolio_id, Portfolio.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    76	        .first()
    77	    )
    78	    if not portfolio:
    79	        raise HTTPException(
    80	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    81	            detail="Portfolio not found in this tenant",
    82	        )
    83	
    84	    # Validate strategy exists and is accessible
    85	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == body.strategy_id).first()
    86	    if not strategy:
    87	        raise HTTPException(
    88	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
    89	        )
    90	    if strategy.owner_type == "TENANT" and strategy.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    91	        raise HTTPException(
    92	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    93	            detail="Strategy not accessible to this tenant",
    94	        )
    95	
    96	    config = StrategyConfig(
    97	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
    98	        portfolio_id=body.portfolio_id,
    99	        strategy_id=body.strategy_id,
   100	        parameters=body.parameters,
   101	        lifecycle_status=body.lifecycle_status,
   102	    )
   103	    db.add(config)
   104	    db.flush()
   105	
   106	    record_audit(
   107	        db,
   108	        action="strategy_config_created",
   109	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   110	        user_id=current_user.id,
   111	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config.id}",
   112	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   113	    )
   114	    db.commit()
   115	    db.refresh(config)
   116	    return config
   117

### services/api-gateway/app/routers/backtests.py
     1	"""Backtest run endpoints (tenant-scoped)."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import BacktestRun, Portfolio, StrategyConfig, User
    14	from app.schemas import BacktestRunCreate, BacktestRunResponse
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests", tags=["backtests"])
    17	
    18	
    19	def _check_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user: User) -> None:
    20	    """Raise 403 if user is not platform admin and does not belong to the tenant."""
    21	    if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN" and current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    22	        raise HTTPException(
    23	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
    24	        )
    25	
    26	
    27	@router.get("", response_model=list[BacktestRunResponse])
    28	def list_backtests(
    29	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    30	    portfolio_id: uuid.UUID | None = Query(default=None),
    31	    backtest_status: str | None = Query(default=None, alias="status"),
    32	    limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=500),
    33	    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    34	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    35	    current_user: User = Depends(
    36	        require_role(
    37	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    38	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
    39	            "TRADER",
    40	            "RESEARCHER",
    41	            "RISK_MANAGER",
    42	            "VIEWER",
    43	        )
    44	    ),
    45	):
    46	    """List backtests for a tenant. Optional filters: portfolio_id, status."""
    47	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    48	
    49	    q = db.query(BacktestRun).filter(BacktestRun.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    50	    if portfolio_id:
    51	        q = q.filter(BacktestRun.portfolio_id == portfolio_id)
    52	    if backtest_status:
    53	        q = q.filter(BacktestRun.status == backtest_status)
    54	
    55	    return q.order_by(BacktestRun.created_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit).all()
    56	
    57	
    58	@router.post(
    59	    "", response_model=BacktestRunResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
    60	)
    61	def create_backtest(
    62	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    63	    body: BacktestRunCreate,
    64	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    65	    current_user: User = Depends(
    66	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER", "RESEARCHER")
    67	    ),
    68	):
    69	    """Create a new backtest run. Status starts as PENDING."""
    70	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    71	
    72	    # Validate portfolio belongs to this tenant
    73	    portfolio = (
    74	        db.query(Portfolio)
    75	        .filter(Portfolio.id == body.portfolio_id, Portfolio.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    76	        .first()
    77	    )
    78	    if not portfolio:
    79	        raise HTTPException(
    80	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    81	            detail="Portfolio not found in this tenant",
    82	        )
    83	
    84	    # Validate strategy config belongs to this tenant
    85	    config = (
    86	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
    87	        .filter(
    88	            StrategyConfig.id == body.strategy_config_id,
    89	            StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id,
    90	        )
    91	        .first()
    92	    )
    93	    if not config:
    94	        raise HTTPException(
    95	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    96	            detail="Strategy config not found in this tenant",
    97	        )
    98	
    99	    if body.start_date >= body.end_date:
   100	        raise HTTPException(
   101	            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
   102	            detail="start_date must be before end_date",
   103	        )
   104	
   105	    backtest = BacktestRun(
   106	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   107	        portfolio_id=body.portfolio_id,
   108	        strategy_config_id=body.strategy_config_id,
   109	        start_date=body.start_date,
   110	        end_date=body.end_date,
   111	        timeframe=body.timeframe,
   112	        parameters=body.parameters,
   113	    )
   114	    db.add(backtest)
   115	    db.flush()
   116	
   117	    record_audit(
   118	        db,
   119	        action="backtest_created",
   120	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   121	        user_id=current_user.id,
   122	        resource=f"backtest:{backtest.id}",
   123	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   124	    )
   125	    db.commit()
   126	    db.refresh(backtest)
   127	    return backtest
   128	
   129	
   130	@router.get("/{backtest_id}", response_model=BacktestRunResponse)
   131	def get_backtest(
   132	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   133	    backtest_id: uuid.UUID,
   134	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   135	    current_user: User = Depends(
   136	        require_role(
   137	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
   138	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
   139	            "TRADER",
   140	            "RESEARCHER",
   141	            "RISK_MANAGER",
   142	            "VIEWER",
   143	        )
   144	    ),
   145	):
   146	    """Get a single backtest run with metrics."""
   147	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   148	
   149	    backtest = (
   150	        db.query(BacktestRun)
   151	        .filter(BacktestRun.id == backtest_id, BacktestRun.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   152	        .first()
   153	    )
   154	    if not backtest:
   155	        raise HTTPException(
   156	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Backtest not found"
   157	        )
   158	    return backtest

     1	"""Aegis Trader - API Gateway service."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
     6	
     7	from fastapi import FastAPI
     8	
     9	from app.database import Base, engine
    10	from app.routers import (
    11	    auth,
    12	    audit,
    13	    backtests,
    14	    instruments,
    15	    ohlcv,
    16	    portfolios,
    17	    strategies,
    18	    strategy_configs,
    19	    tenants,
    20	    users,
    21	)
    22	
    23	
    24	@asynccontextmanager
    25	async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    26	    """Create database tables on startup."""
    27	    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    28	    yield
    29	
    30	
    31	app = FastAPI(title="Aegis Trader API", version="0.1.0", lifespan=lifespan)
    32	
    33	
    34	# Health check (no auth required)
    35	@app.get("/health")
    36	async def health():
    37	    return {"status": "ok"}
    38	
    39

    41	app.include_router(auth.router, prefix="/api")

### auth
     1	"""JWT authentication, password hashing, and FastAPI dependencies."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
     7	
     8	from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
     9	from fastapi.security import HTTPAuthorizationCredentials, HTTPBearer
    10	from jose import JWTError, jwt
    11	from passlib.context import CryptContext
    12	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
    13	
    14	from app.config import settings
    15	from app.database import get_db
    16	
    17	pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
    18	bearer_scheme = HTTPBearer()
    19	
    20	VALID_ROLES = {
    21	    "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    22	    "TENANT_ADMIN",
    23	    "TRADER",
    24	    "RESEARCHER",
    25	    "RISK_MANAGER",
    26	    "VIEWER",
    27	}
    28	VALID_TENANT_STATUSES = {"ACTIVE", "SUSPENDED", "READ_ONLY", "DISABLED"}
    29	VALID_TRADING_MODES = {"BACKTEST", "REPLAY", "PAPER", "SHADOW", "LIVE"}
    30	
    31	
    32	def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    33	    """Hash a plaintext password."""
    34	    return pwd_context.hash(password)
    35	
    36	
    37	def verify_password(plain: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
    38	    """Verify a plaintext password against a hash."""
    39	    return pwd_context.verify(plain, hashed)
    40	
    41	
    42	def create_access_token(
    43	    user_id: uuid.UUID, tenant_id: uuid.UUID | None, role: str
    44	) -> str:
    45	    """Create a signed JWT access token."""
    46	    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(minutes=settings.jwt_expire_minutes)
    47	    payload = {
    48	        "sub": str(user_id),
    49	        "tenant_id": str(tenant_id) if tenant_id else None,
    50	        "role": role,
    51	        "exp": expire,
    52	    }
    53	    return jwt.encode(payload, settings.jwt_secret, algorithm=settings.jwt_algorithm)
    54	
    55	
    56	def get_current_user(
    57	    creds: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(bearer_scheme),
    58	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    59	):
    60	    """FastAPI dependency: decode JWT and return the User row."""
    61	    from app.models import User
    62	
    63	    try:
    64	        payload = jwt.decode(
    65	            creds.credentials, settings.jwt_secret, algorithms=[settings.jwt_algorithm]
    66	        )
    67	    except JWTError:
    68	        raise HTTPException(
    69	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid token"
    70	        )
    71	
    72	    user_id = payload.get("sub")
    73	    if not user_id:
    74	        raise HTTPException(
    75	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid token payload"
    76	        )
    77	
    78	    user = (
    79	        db.query(User)
    80	        .filter(User.id == uuid.UUID(user_id), User.is_active.is_(True))
    81	        .first()
    82	    )
    83	    if not user:
    84	        raise HTTPException(
    85	            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    86	            detail="User not found or inactive",
    87	        )
    88	    return user
    89	
    90	
    91	def require_role(*allowed_roles: str):
    92	    """FastAPI dependency factory: restrict endpoint to specific roles."""
    93	
    94	    def checker(current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    95	        if current_user.role not in allowed_roles:
    96	            raise HTTPException(
    97	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Insufficient permissions"
    98	            )
    99	        return current_user
   100	
   101	    return checker
   102	
   103	
   104	def require_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
   105	    """FastAPI dependency: ensure the user belongs to the requested tenant (or is platform admin)."""
   106	    if current_user.role == "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   107	        return current_user
   108	    if current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
   109	        raise HTTPException(
   110	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied to this tenant"
   111	        )
   112	    return current_user
### database
     1	"""SQLAlchemy engine, session factory, and declarative base."""
     2	
     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	from collections.abc import Generator
     6	
     7	from sqlalchemy import create_engine
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Session, sessionmaker
     9	
    10	from app.config import settings
    11	
    12	engine = create_engine(settings.database_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    13	SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)
    14	
    15	
    16	class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    17	    pass
    18	
    19	
    20	def get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    21	    """FastAPI dependency that yields a database session."""
    22	    db = SessionLocal()
    23	    try:
    24	        yield db
    25	    finally:
    26	        db.close()

    14	from app.schemas import (
    15	    StrategyConfigCreate,
    16	    StrategyConfigResponse,
    17	    StrategyConfigUpdate,
    18	)

    20	router = APIRouter(
    21	    prefix="/tenants/{tenant_id}/strategy-configs", tags=["strategy-configs"]
    22	)
    23	
    24	
    25	def _check_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user: User) -> None:
    26	    """Raise 403 if user is not platform admin and does not belong to the tenant."""
    27	    if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN" and current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    28	        raise HTTPException(
    29	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
    30	        )

    33	@router.get("", response_model=list[StrategyConfigResponse])
    34	def list_strategy_configs(
    35	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    36	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    37	    current_user: User = Depends(
    38	        require_role(
    39	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    40	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
    41	            "TRADER",
    42	            "RESEARCHER",
    43	            "RISK_MANAGER",
    44	            "VIEWER",
    45	        )
    46	    ),
    47	):
    48	    """List strategy configs for a tenant."""
    49	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    50	    return (
    51	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
    52	        .filter(StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    53	        .order_by(StrategyConfig.created_at)
    54	        .all()
    55	    )

    58	@router.post(
    59	    "", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
    60	)
    61	def create_strategy_config(
    62	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    63	    body: StrategyConfigCreate,
    64	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    65	    current_user: User = Depends(
    66	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER")
    67	    ),
    68	):
    69	    """Create a strategy config within a tenant."""
    70	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    71	
    72	    # Validate portfolio belongs to this tenant
    73	    portfolio = (
    74	        db.query(Portfolio)
    75	        .filter(Portfolio.id == body.portfolio_id, Portfolio.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    76	        .first()
    77	    )
    78	    if not portfolio:
    79	        raise HTTPException(
    80	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    81	            detail="Portfolio not found in this tenant",
    82	        )
    83	
    84	    # Validate strategy exists and is accessible
    85	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == body.strategy_id).first()
    86	    if not strategy:
    87	        raise HTTPException(
    88	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
    89	        )
    90	    if strategy.owner_type == "TENANT" and strategy.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    91	        raise HTTPException(
    92	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    93	            detail="Strategy not accessible to this tenant",
    94	        )
    95	
    96	    config = StrategyConfig(
    97	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
    98	        portfolio_id=body.portfolio_id,
    99	        strategy_id=body.strategy_id,
   100	        parameters=body.parameters,
   101	        lifecycle_status=body.lifecycle_status,
   102	    )
   103	    db.add(config)
   104	    db.flush()
   105	
   106	    record_audit(
   107	        db,
   108	        action="strategy_config_created",
   109	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   110	        user_id=current_user.id,
   111	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config.id}",
   112	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   113	    )
   114	    db.commit()
   115	    db.refresh(config)
   116	    return config
   117	
   118	
   119	@router.get("/{config_id}", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse)

   127	            "TENANT_ADMIN",

   129	            "RESEARCHER",
   130	            "RISK_MANAGER",

   138	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
   139	        .filter(StrategyConfig.id == config_id, StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   140	        .first()
   141	    )

   143	        raise HTTPException(

   149	@router.patch("/{config_id}", response_model=StrategyConfigResponse)
   150	def update_strategy_config(
   151	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   152	    config_id: uuid.UUID,
   153	    body: StrategyConfigUpdate,
   154	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   155	    current_user: User = Depends(
   156	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER")
   157	    ),
   158	):
   159	    """Update a strategy config."""
   160	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   161	    config = (
   162	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
   163	        .filter(StrategyConfig.id == config_id, StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   164	        .first()
   165	    )
   166	    if not config:
   167	        raise HTTPException(
   168	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy config not found"
   169	        )
   170	
   171	    before = {
   172	        "parameters": config.parameters,
   173	        "lifecycle_status": config.lifecycle_status,
   174	        "is_active": config.is_active,
   175	    }
   176	    updates = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
   177	    for key, value in updates.items():
   178	        setattr(config, key, value)
   179	    db.flush()
   180	
   181	    record_audit(
   182	        db,
   183	        action="strategy_config_updated",
   184	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   185	        user_id=current_user.id,
   186	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config_id}",
   187	        before_state=before,
   188	        after_state=updates,
   189	    )
   190	    db.commit()
   191	    db.refresh(config)
   192	    return config
   193	
   194	
   195	@router.delete("/{config_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
   196	def delete_strategy_config(
   197	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   198	    config_id: uuid.UUID,
   199	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   200	    current_user: User = Depends(require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN")),
   201	):
   202	    """Delete a strategy config."""
   203	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
   204	    config = (
   205	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
   206	        .filter(StrategyConfig.id == config_id, StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   207	        .first()
   208	    )
   209	    if not config:
   210	        raise HTTPException(
   211	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy config not found"
   212	        )
   213	
   214	    record_audit(
   215	        db,
   216	        action="strategy_config_deleted",
   217	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   218	        user_id=current_user.id,
   219	        resource=f"strategy_config:{config_id}",
   220	        before_state={
   221	            "parameters": config.parameters,
   222	            "lifecycle_status": config.lifecycle_status,
   223	            "is_active": config.is_active,
   224	        },
   225	    )
   226	    db.delete(config)
   227	    db.commit()
     1	"""Backtest run endpoints (tenant-scoped)."""

     3	from __future__ import annotations
     4	
     5	import uuid
     6	
     7	from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
     8	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
     9	
    10	from app.auth import require_role
    11	from app.audit import record_audit
    12	from app.database import get_db
    13	from app.models import BacktestRun, Portfolio, StrategyConfig, User
    14	from app.schemas import BacktestRunCreate, BacktestRunResponse
    15	
    16	router = APIRouter(prefix="/tenants/{tenant_id}/backtests", tags=["backtests"])
    17	
    18	
    19	def _check_tenant_access(tenant_id: uuid.UUID, current_user: User) -> None:
    20	    """Raise 403 if user is not platform admin and does not belong to the tenant."""
    21	    if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN" and current_user.tenant_id != tenant_id:
    22	        raise HTTPException(
    23	            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
    24	        )

    27	@router.get("", response_model=list[BacktestRunResponse])
    28	def list_backtests(
    29	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    30	    portfolio_id: uuid.UUID | None = Query(default=None),
    31	    backtest_status: str | None = Query(default=None, alias="status"),
    32	    limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=500),
    33	    offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
    34	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    35	    current_user: User = Depends(
    36	        require_role(
    37	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
    38	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
    39	            "TRADER",
    40	            "RESEARCHER",
    41	            "RISK_MANAGER",
    42	            "VIEWER",
    43	        )
    44	    ),
    45	):
    46	    """List backtests for a tenant. Optional filters: portfolio_id, status."""
    47	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    48	
    49	    q = db.query(BacktestRun).filter(BacktestRun.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    50	    if portfolio_id:
    51	        q = q.filter(BacktestRun.portfolio_id == portfolio_id)
    52	    if backtest_status:
    53	        q = q.filter(BacktestRun.status == backtest_status)
    54	
    55	    return q.order_by(BacktestRun.created_at.desc()).offset(offset).limit(limit).all()
    56	
    57	
    58	@router.post(
    59	    "", response_model=BacktestRunResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED
    60	)
    61	def create_backtest(
    62	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
    63	    body: BacktestRunCreate,
    64	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    65	    current_user: User = Depends(
    66	        require_role("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "TRADER", "RESEARCHER")
    67	    ),
    68	):
    69	    """Create a new backtest run. Status starts as PENDING."""
    70	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)
    71	
    72	    # Validate portfolio belongs to this tenant
    73	    portfolio = (
    74	        db.query(Portfolio)
    75	        .filter(Portfolio.id == body.portfolio_id, Portfolio.tenant_id == tenant_id)
    76	        .first()
    77	    )
    78	    if not portfolio:
    79	        raise HTTPException(
    80	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    81	            detail="Portfolio not found in this tenant",
    82	        )
    83	
    84	    # Validate strategy config belongs to this tenant
    85	    config = (
    86	        db.query(StrategyConfig)
    87	        .filter(
    88	            StrategyConfig.id == body.strategy_config_id,
    89	            StrategyConfig.tenant_id == tenant_id,
    90	        )
    91	        .first()
    92	    )
    93	    if not config:
    94	        raise HTTPException(
    95	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
    96	            detail="Strategy config not found in this tenant",
    97	        )
    98	
    99	    if body.start_date >= body.end_date:
   100	        raise HTTPException(
   101	            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
   102	            detail="start_date must be before end_date",
   103	        )
   104	
   105	    backtest = BacktestRun(
   106	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   107	        portfolio_id=body.portfolio_id,
   108	        strategy_config_id=body.strategy_config_id,
   109	        start_date=body.start_date,
   110	        end_date=body.end_date,
   111	        timeframe=body.timeframe,
   112	        parameters=body.parameters,
   113	    )
   114	    db.add(backtest)
   115	    db.flush()
   116	
   117	    record_audit(
   118	        db,
   119	        action="backtest_created",
   120	        tenant_id=tenant_id,
   121	        user_id=current_user.id,
   122	        resource=f"backtest:{backtest.id}",
   123	        after_state=body.model_dump(mode="json"),
   124	    )
   125	    db.commit()
   126	    db.refresh(backtest)
   127	    return backtest
   128	
   129	
   130	@router.get("/{backtest_id}", response_model=BacktestRunResponse)
   131	def get_backtest(
   132	    tenant_id: uuid.UUID,
   133	    backtest_id: uuid.UUID,
   134	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   135	    current_user: User = Depends(
   136	        require_role(
   137	            "PLATFORM_ADMIN",
   138	            "TENANT_ADMIN",
   139	            "TRADER",
   140	            "RESEARCHER",
   141	            "RISK_MANAGER",
   142	            "VIEWER",
   143	        )
   144	    ),
   145	):
   146	    """Get a single backtest run with metrics."""
   147	    _check_tenant_access(tenant_id, current_user)

   149	    backtest = (
   150	        db.query(BacktestRun)
   151	        .filter(BacktestRun.id == backtest_id, BacktestRun.tenant_id == tenant_id)
   152	        .first()
   153	    )
   154	    if not backtest:
   155	        raise HTTPException(
   156	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Backtest not found"
   157	        )
   158	    return backtest
     1	"""Shared pytest fixtures for the api-gateway test suite.
     2	
     3	The suite runs against a real PostgreSQL database (never the live one): the
     4	``DATABASE_URL`` environment variable is read *before* the application is
     5	imported so that ``app.database`` binds its engine to the test database.  The
     6	schema is dropped and recreated once per test session; each test function gets
     7	its own session while the tables (and committed rows) persist for the whole
     8	session.
     9	
    10	Typical usage::
    11	
    12	    def test_health(client):
    13	        assert client.get("/api/tenants").status_code == 401
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import os
    19	import sys
    20	import uuid
    21	from collections.abc import Generator
    22	from pathlib import Path
    23	
    24	# ``conftest.py`` lives at ``<service>/tests/conftest.py`` but the application
    25	# is imported as the top-level ``app`` package.  Make the service root
    26	# importable so the suite works no matter where pytest is invoked from.
    27	SERVICE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    28	if str(SERVICE_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    29	    sys.path.insert(0, str(SERVICE_ROOT))
    30	
    31	# Must be set before importing anything from ``app``.
    32	os.environ.setdefault("DATABASE_URL", "postgresql://aegis:aegis@localhost:5432/aegis")
    33	os.environ.setdefault("JWT_SECRET", "test-jwt-secret")
    34	
    35	import httpx  # noqa: E402
    36	import pytest  # noqa: E402
    37	from fastapi.testclient import TestClient  # noqa: E402
    38	from sqlalchemy import create_engine  # noqa: E402
    39	from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker  # noqa: E402
    40	
    41	from app.auth import create_access_token  # noqa: E402
    42	from app.config import settings  # noqa: E402
    43	from app.database import Base, get_db  # noqa: E402
    44	from main import app  # noqa: E402
    45	
    46	# Dedicated credentials for the platform admin bootstrapped by ``admin_token``.
    47	ADMIN_EMAIL = "admin@example.com"
    48	ADMIN_PASSWORD = "admin-test-pass"
    49	
    50	
    51	@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
    52	def engine():
    53	    """SQLAlchemy engine bound to the test database."""
    54	    engine = create_engine(settings.database_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    55	    try:
    56	        yield engine
    57	    finally:
    58	        engine.dispose()
    59	
    60	
    61	@pytest.fixture(scope="session", autouse=True)
    62	def _reset_database(engine):
    63	    """Drop and recreate every table once, giving the suite a clean slate."""
    64	    Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=engine)
    65	    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    66	    yield
    67	    # Best-effort cleanup; failures here must not fail the suite.
    68	    try:
    69	        Base.metadata.drop_all(bind=engine)
    70	    except Exception:  # pragma: no cover - defensive teardown
    71	        pass
    72	
    73	
    74	@pytest.fixture
    75	def db_session(engine) -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    76	    """A fresh ORM session for tests that need to inspect or seed the DB.
    77	
    78	    The session is intentionally *not* closed by the request override below, so
    79	    callers may keep using it after an API call; this fixture owns its lifetime.
    80	    """
    81	    session = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)()
    82	    try:
    83	        yield session
    84	    finally:
    85	        session.close()
    86	
    87	
    88	@pytest.fixture
    89	def client(engine) -> Generator[httpx.Client, None, None]:
    90	    """A synchronous httpx client wired to the FastAPI app.
    91	
    92	    ``TestClient`` is an ``httpx.Client`` subclass, so tests get the usual httpx
    93	    API.  ``get_db`` is overridden to hand every request a session bound to the
    94	    test engine instead of the application's configured database.
    95	    """
    96	
    97	    def override_get_db() -> Generator[Session, None, None]:
    98	        db = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)()
    99	        try:
   100	            yield db
   101	        finally:
   102	            db.close()
   103	
   104	    app.dependency_overrides[get_db] = override_get_db
   105	    # The session fixture prepares the schema; entering the client also runs the
   106	    # app lifespan (whose ``create_all`` is idempotent).
   107	    with TestClient(app) as test_client:
   108	        yield test_client
   109	    app.dependency_overrides.clear()
   110	
   111	
   112	def _ensure_platform_admin(engine) -> str:
   113	    """Return a platform-admin JWT, creating the admin on first use.
   114	
   115	    Prefers the real ``/api/auth/bootstrap`` endpoint so callers exercise the
   116	    bootstrap flow.  On later calls, when the platform is already bootstrapped,
   117	    the existing admin is looked up and a token is minted directly.
   118	    """
   119	    from app.models import User
   120	
   121	    session_factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False)
   122	    with TestClient(app) as api:
   123	        response = api.post(
   124	            "/api/auth/bootstrap",
   125	            json={"email": ADMIN_EMAIL, "password": ADMIN_PASSWORD},
   126	        )
   127	        if response.status_code == 201:
   128	            return response.json()["access_token"]
   129	
   130	    assert response.status_code == 409, response.text
   131	    with session_factory() as db:
   132	        admin = db.query(User).filter_by(email=ADMIN_EMAIL).one()
   133	        return create_access_token(admin.id, admin.tenant_id, admin.role)
   134	
   135	
   136	@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
   137	def admin_token(engine) -> str:
   138	    """JWT for a bootstrapped platform admin (created once per session)."""
   139	    return _ensure_platform_admin(engine)
   140	
   141	
   142	@pytest.fixture
   143	def admin_headers(admin_token) -> dict[str, str]:
   144	    """Authorization header for the bootstrapped platform admin."""
   145	    return {"Authorization": f"Bearer {admin_token}"}
   146

   148	@pytest.fixture

   185	        "password": admin_password,

   187	        "headers": {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
   188	    }

tasks/codex-review.KBBwbT.md:7:> `[P2] Bind development data ports to loopback` — The documented dev command publishes PostgreSQL, Redis, and MinIO on every host interface. Redis is especially exposed because protected mode is disabled and no password or ACL is configured, giving any reachable client full command access, including data reads and `FLUSHALL`. Use loopback bindings or authenticate every published service. [docker-compose.dev.yml:2](/home/udai/personal/aegis/docker-compose.dev.yml:2), [docker-compose.dev.yml:5](/home/udai/personal/aegis/docker-compose.dev.yml:5), [redis.conf:11](/home/udai/personal/aegis/infra/redis/redis.conf:11), [docker-compose.yml:124](/home/udai/personal/aegis/docker-compose.yml:124). Docker documents that [omitting a host IP binds all interfaces](https://docs.docker.com/reference/compose-file/services/#ports).
tasks/codex-review.KBBwbT.md:11:> `[P2] Prevent cache eviction from deleting coordination state` — Redis is designated for locks and queues, but `allkeys-lru` permits eviction of every key when the 512 MB limit is reached. Memory pressure can therefore silently remove a lock, queued job, or temporary trading state rather than returning an error, causing duplicate work or lost jobs. Separate disposable cache data from coordination state or use a non-evicting policy for the latter. [redis.conf:8](/home/udai/personal/aegis/infra/redis/redis.conf:8), [PRD §59](/home/udai/personal/aegis/aegis_trader_multi_tenant_prd.md:1548). Redis documents that [`allkeys-lru` evicts arbitrary least-recently-used keys](https://redis.io/docs/latest/develop/reference/eviction/).

Loop until verified.**\n\nTransform tasks into verifiable goals:\n- \"Add validation\" → \"Write tests for invalid inputs, then make them pass\"\n- \"Fix the bug\" → \"Write a test that reproduces it, then make it pass\"\n- \"Refactor X\" → \"Ensure tests pass before and after\"\n\nFor multi-step tasks, state a brief plan:\n```\n1. [Step] → verify: [check]\n2. [Step] → verify: [check]\n3. [Step] → verify: [check]\n```\n\nStrong success criteria let you loop independently. Weak criteria (\"make it work\") require constant clarification.\n\n---\n\n**These guidelines are working if:** fewer unnecessary changes in diffs, fewer rewrites due to overcomplication, and clarifying questions come before implementation rather than after mistakes."}]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"e8817709-1114-4b86-be1c-5b1286039d05","timestamp":"2026-09-12T03:29:10.092Z","rendered":[{"content":"<system-reminder>\nCodebase and user instructions are shown below. It delegates\nproduction edits and verification to you — edits to keep its own context on the\ndiagnosis, verification because **the author of a change must never be its only\nwitness.** Everything it knows about your run comes from what you report, so the\naccuracy of your report is the whole job.\n\nA brief will be one of four shapes. Read which one you got before starting:\n\n- **Implement** — make a designed change, then validate it.\n- **Investigate** — gather facts. Change nothing.\n- **Verify** — run named commands and report literal output. Change nothing.\n- **Git/release** — execute named git steps exactly as spelled out.\n\nReport in every case: files inspected; files changed (none, under investigate or\nverify); implementation details; commands run with exact command lines; results as\nliteral output — never a summary like \"tests pass\"; assumptions; unresolved risks.\n\n## Persona (Implement briefs only)\n\nAn Implement brief may name a persona: one of the ~45 domain-specialist files\nunder `.claude/agents/` (e.g. `frontend-developer`, `sql-pro`,\n`security-engineer`). If it does, `Read` that file first and apply its domain\nexpertise and checklists to how you design the change. It is domain knowledge\nlayered on top of your job, never a replacement for it — every rule in this\nfile (scope, reporting, git safety, the four brief shapes) still governs, even\nwhere the persona file says something else about how to work or report.\n\n## Scope\n\nThe coordinator has already diagnosed the problem and designed the fix. Your job\nis faithful execution, not redesign.\n\n- Stay inside the files and symbols the brief names. Found something else that\n  looks wrong? Report it, don't fix it.\n- No refactors, reformatting, dependency changes, or cleanups that weren't asked\n  for. Unrequested edits are the main way delegation goes wrong.\n- Follow repo conventions exactly as documented in this project's CLAUDE.md —\n  naming, module layout, architectural layering, testing conventions, and any\n  domain-specific rules it calls out. Don't assume conventions from a\n  different project apply here.\n- If the brief is ambiguous, contradicts the code, or rests on a wrong\n  assumption, **stop and report** instead of guessing.\n\n## Commands and output\n\nCommand output is the coordinator's only window onto what happened. Treat it as\nthe deliverable.\n\n- Run the commands the brief names, in the working directory it names. Don't\n  substitute one you think is equivalent — if the named command is wrong or\n  missing, say so and stop.\n- Paste output **literally**: exact command line, real stdout/stderr,\n  pass/fail/skip counts, exit code. Never retype, tidy, translate, or re-order.\n- Too large? Paste head and tail and say explicitly that you truncated it, and\n  where. Never silently trim.\n- Quote every failure verbatim, including stack traces and assertion diffs.\n  Report failing tests as failing — a failure you surface is useful, one you hide\n  is a defect shipped.\n- Don't retry a failing command with different flags to get a green result. A\n  failure is a finding.\n- If a command was skipped, timed out, or couldn't run, say which and why. A gap\n  you declare costs one round trip; a gap you paper over ships a defect.\n- Never claim a command passed without running it, and never describe output you\n  didn't capture. If you lost it, re-run or say so.\n\n## Investigate briefs\n\nYou are gathering evidence for someone else's diagnosis. Facts, not verdicts.\n\n- `file:line` references with verbatim excerpts. Paraphrase loses exactly the\n  detail the diagnosis turns on.\n- If something the brief asked about doesn't exist, say so explicitly — \"no\n  matches for X in Y\" is a result, and silence reads as unchecked.\n- You may state a hypothesis, clearly labelled. Never present it as the cause.\n- Fix nothing, even something obviously broken.\n\n## Verify briefs\n\nYour value is that you have no stake in the result — so if the code is wrong, say\nso. **Make no edits, stage nothing, commit nothing**; if a command fails because\nof a defect, report it, don't repair it.\n\n- Run every listed command in order, even if an earlier one fails, unless the\n  brief says stop on failure.\n- Paste literal diff output when asked, unabridged.\n- State plainly if what you observe contradicts the brief's expectations. That\n  contradiction is the most valuable thing you can report.\n\n## Revert-and-rerun drills\n\nA brief may ask you to prove a test genuinely fails without its fix. This is the\none case where you deliberately break working code, so the restore matters more\nthan the experiment.\n\n1. Paste the file's checksum (e.g. `sha256sum <file>`) and confirm it holds no\n   other uncommitted work you're about to disturb.\n2. Revert **only** the named behaviour, using the literal text the brief gives.\n3. Run the named test; paste the failing output verbatim. A failure here is the\n   expected result, not something to fix.\n4. Restore, re-run, paste the passing output plus the checksum a second time.\n\n**The two checksums must match** — that, and only that, proves the restore was\nbyte-exact. Don't substitute `git diff`: the fix under test is usually itself\nuncommitted, so a non-empty diff against `HEAD` is expected and proves nothing\neither way. If the checksums differ, stop and say so loudly. If the test\n**passes** while the fix is reverted, report exactly that — it is the most\nvaluable thing you can find.\n\n## Codex review runs\n\nWhen the brief asks you to run `.claude/bin/codex-reviewer` (resolve it relative\nto the repo root, e.g. `\"$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)/.claude/bin/codex-reviewer\"`\n— never assume it's at a fixed absolute path):\n\n- Pass the prompt text through **unchanged** — don't shorten, rewrite, or add.\n- The wrapper prints a temp-file path on stderr. Report that path **verbatim**\n  plus the exit code; the coordinator reads the file itself.\n- Don't summarize, interpret, or act on the findings. Relaying a précis instead of\n  the path defeats the point of an external review.\n- If no path was printed, say so — don't guess a location.\n\n## Git and releases\n\nOnly when the brief tells you to, and only the steps it specifies. Never decide on\nyour own to commit, push, merge, tag, or release.\n\n- **Stage only the exact paths the brief lists.** Never `git add -A`, `git add .`,\n  or a glob. The tree may hold private artifacts (pasted images, statement PDFs,\n  `data/`, `.claude/`, `CLAUDE.md`) that must never be committed — staging one is\n  the worst outcome of this job.\n- Before committing, run `git diff --cached --name-only` and check it matches the\n  brief's list exactly. Anything extra: **stop and report**.\n- **If the brief says stage only, stop after staging** — however obviously next a\n  commit looks. The coordinator approves the staged list between those steps and\n  can't once you've committed. Paste `git status --porcelain`,\n  `git diff --cached --name-only`, and `git diff --cached --stat`, then end.\n- Use the commit message, PR body, branch, and tag verbatim, including the\n  `Co-Authored-By: Claude ...` / Claude Code trailers.\n- Never rewrite history: no `reset --hard`, `rebase`, `commit --amend`,\n  `push --force`, or `stash drop` unless the brief names it.\n- Paste the real output of each git command.\n\n## Never\n\n- Delete or move files the brief doesn't name.\n- Any git operation the brief didn't ask for — including \"helpful\" cleanup, branch\n  deletion, or pulling/rebasing to resolve a conflict.\n- Touch production, remote hosts, or live databases.\n- Install packages or edit lockfiles unless explicitly asked.\n- Edit any file under an investigate or verify brief.\n\nYour report is treated as untrusted evidence and will be checked against the files\nthemselves and a re-run by a different worker — so inaccuracy is caught and just\nwastes a round trip. .claude/plugins/marketplaces/claude-plugins-official/plugins/code-modernization/README.md:65:- **`/modernize-uplift <system-dir> <source-version> <target-version> [project-pattern]`** — Same-stack version bump (e.g. `.NET Framework 4.8` → `.NET 8`, Spring Boot 2 → 3) — the common case `transform` gets wrong by rewriting. Preserves the code and makes the smallest diffs that compile and behave identically, driven by a **delta catalog** (the known breaking changes that *this* code actually hits) and the ecosystem's migration tooling. Equivalence is proven by running the test suite on both the old and new runtime where both can run here (otherwise it falls back to characterization tests, like `transform`). Migration is **pilot-first**: one representative project is migrated end-to-end in-session and its lessons written to a `PLAYBOOK.md` before anything else is touched; the rest then fan out, one agent per project, in **dependency-aware escalating batches behind a circuit breaker**. Produces `DELTA_CATALOG.md`, `BASELINE.md`, `PLAYBOOK.md` + `UPLIFT_NOTES.md`. If the catalog shows most of the code is forced to change, it tells you to use `transform` instead.
.claude/plugins/marketplaces/claude-plugins-official/plugins/code-modernization/README.md:67:- **`/modernize-harden <system-dir>`** — Security pass on the **legacy** system: OWASP/CWE, dependency CVEs, secrets, injection. Produces `SECURITY_FINDINGS.md` (ranked) and a reviewed `security_remediation.patch`. **Never edits `legacy/`** — you review and apply the patch yourself. Useful while the legacy system keeps running in production during migration.
.claude/plugins/marketplaces/claude-plugins-official/plugins/code-modernization/README.md:80:- **`version-delta-analyst`** — Finds the breaking changes between two versions of one stack that bite *this* codebase, and drives the ecosystem migration tool. *(uplift)*
.claude/plugins/marketplaces/claude-plugins-official/plugins/code-modernization/README.md:119:On Claude Code builds with the Workflow tool, five commands (`extract-rules`, `harden`, `assess --portfolio`, `reimagine`, `uplift`) run as scripted multi-agent orchestrations that fan out more agents for deeper coverage — looping until findings stabilize, and adversarially verifying each finding before it's written. `uplift`'s migration fan-out runs in dependency-aware escalating batches behind a per-batch **circuit breaker**, so a playbook that stops working is caught within a handful of agents and the spend stops until it is revised. They fall back to direct subagent fan-out on older builds automatically; no configuration needed. Invoking the slash command is the opt-in.

   117	@router.get("/{strategy_id}", response_model=StrategyResponse)
   118	def get_strategy(
   119	    strategy_id: uuid.UUID,
   120	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   121	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   122	):
   123	    """Get a single strategy. Checks visibility."""
   124	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == strategy_id).first()
   125	    if not strategy:
   126	        raise HTTPException(
   127	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
   128	        )
   129	
   130	    if strategy.owner_type == "TENANT" and current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   131	        if current_user.tenant_id != strategy.tenant_id:
   132	            raise HTTPException(
   133	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
   134	            )
   135	
   136	    return strategy
   137	
   138	
   139	@router.patch("/{strategy_id}", response_model=StrategyResponse)
   140	def update_strategy(
   141	    strategy_id: uuid.UUID,
   142	    body: StrategyUpdate,
   143	    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
   144	    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   145	):
   146	    """Update a strategy. Ownership check enforced."""
   147	    strategy = db.query(Strategy).filter(Strategy.id == strategy_id).first()
   148	    if not strategy:
   149	        raise HTTPException(
   150	            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Strategy not found"
   151	        )
   152	
   153	    # Ownership check
   154	    if strategy.owner_type == "PLATFORM":
   155	        if current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN":
   156	            raise HTTPException(
   157	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
   158	                detail="Only platform admins can update platform strategies",
   159	            )
   160	    else:
   161	        if current_user.role not in ("PLATFORM_ADMIN", "TENANT_ADMIN", "RESEARCHER"):
   162	            raise HTTPException(
   163	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Insufficient permissions"
   164	            )
   165	        if (
   166	            current_user.role != "PLATFORM_ADMIN"
   167	            and current_user.tenant_id != strategy.tenant_id
   168	        ):
   169	            raise HTTPException(
   170	                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Access denied"
   171	            )
   172	
   173	    before = {
   174	        "name": strategy.name,
   175	        "version": strategy.version,
   176	        "status": strategy.status,
   177	    }
   178	    updates = body.model_dump(exclude_unset=True)
   179	    for key, value in updates.items():
   180	        setattr(strategy, key, value)
   181	    db.flush()
   182	
   183	    record_audit(
   184	        db,
   185	        action="strategy_updated",
   186	        tenant_id=strategy.tenant_id,
   187	        user_id=current_user.id,
   188	        resource=f"strategy:{strategy_id}",
   189	        before_state=before,
   190	        after_state=updates,
   191	    )
   192	    db.commit()
   193	    db.refresh(strategy)
   194	    return strategy
     1	"""End-to-end tests for the Phase 2 API endpoints.
     2	
     3	Covers instrument CRUD, OHLCV batch upload/upsert/query, platform and tenant
     4	strategies, tenant-scoped strategy configs, and backtest runs.  All requests

    15	def _suffix() -> str:
    16	    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]
    17	
    18	
    19	def _create_instrument(
    20	    client, admin_headers, symbol: str | None = None, exchange: str = "NSE"
    21	) -> dict:
    22	    """Create an instrument as the platform admin and return the response body."""
    23	    body = {
    24	        "symbol": symbol or f"TST{_suffix()}",
    25	        "exchange": exchange,
    26	        "instrument_type": "EQUITY",
    27	        "name": f"Test Instrument {symbol or _suffix()}",
    28	        "lot_size": 1,
    29	        "tick_size": 0.05,
    30	    }
    31	    response = client.post("/api/instruments", json=body, headers=admin_headers)
    32	    assert response.status_code == 201, f"Instrument create failed: {response.text}"
    33	    return response.json()
    34	
    35	
    36	def _create_researcher(client, tenant_with_admin: dict) -> dict:
    37	    """Create a RESEARCHER user in the tenant and return login details."""
    38	    tenant_id = tenant_with_admin["tenant_id"]
    39	    suffix = _suffix()
    40	    email = f"researcher-{suffix}@example.com"
    41	    password = "researcher-pass"
    42	
    43	    create_response = client.post(
    44	        f"/api/tenants/{tenant_id}/users",
    45	        json={"email": email, "password": password, "role": "RESEARCHER"},
    46	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
    47	    )
    48	    assert (
    49	        create_response.status_code == 201
    50	    ), f"Researcher create failed: {create_response.text}"
    51	
    52	    login_response = client.post(
    53	        "/api/auth/login", json={"email": email, "password": password}
    54	    )
    55	    assert (
    56	        login_response.status_code == 200
    57	    ), f"Researcher login failed: {login_response.text}"
    58	    token = login_response.json()["access_token"]
    59	
    60	    return {
    61	        "user": create_response.json(),
    62	        "email": email,
    63	        "headers": {"Authorization": f"Bearer {token}"},
    64	    }
    65

   215	def test_ohlcv_upload_and_upserted_count(client, admin_headers):
   216	    """Platform admin batch-uploads OHLCV bars; the response reports the count."""
   217	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   218	    base = datetime(2024, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0)
   219	    bars = [
   220	        {

   222	            "timestamp": (base + timedelta(days=i)).isoformat(),
   223	            "open": 100.0 + i,
   224	            "high": 105.0 + i,
   225	            "low": 99.0 + i,
   226	            "close": 102.0 + i,

   228	        }
   229	        for i in range(5)
   230	    ]
   231

   234	    )

   236	    assert response.json() == {

   238	    }, f"Expected upserted=5, got {response.json()}"

   241	def test_ohlcv_upsert_updates_not_duplicates(client, admin_headers):
   242	    """Uploading the same timestamp twice updates the bar instead of duplicating."""
   243	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   244	    ts = datetime(2024, 2, 1, 9, 15, 0).isoformat()
   245	    bar_first = {
   246	        "timeframe": "1d",
   247	        "timestamp": ts,
   248	        "open": 50.0,
   249	        "high": 55.0,
   250	        "low": 49.0,
   251	        "close": 52.0,
   252	        "volume": 100,
   253	    }
   254	    bar_second = {
   255	        "timeframe": "1d",
   256	        "timestamp": ts,
   257	        "open": 50.0,
   258	        "high": 60.0,
   259	        "low": 49.5,
   260	        "close": 58.0,
   261	        "volume": 250,
   262	    }
   263	
   264	    first = client.post(
   265	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   266	        json=[bar_first],
   267	        headers=admin_headers,
   268	    )
   269	    assert first.status_code == 201, f"First upload failed: {first.text}"
   270	
   271	    second = client.post(
   272	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   273	        json=[bar_second],
   274	        headers=admin_headers,
   275	    )
   276	    assert second.status_code == 201, f"Second upload failed: {second.text}"
   277	    assert second.json() == {"upserted": 1}, "Re-upload should report 1 upserted bar"
   278	
   279	    query = client.get(
   280	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv", headers=admin_headers
   281	    )
   282	    assert query.status_code == 200, f"OHLCV query failed: {query.text}"
   283	    bars = query.json()
   284	    assert len(bars) == 1, f"Upsert should not duplicate bars, found {len(bars)}"
   285	    assert (
   286	        bars[0]["close"] == 58.0
   287	    ), f"Bar should hold updated close, got {bars[0]['close']}"
   288	    assert (
   289	        bars[0]["volume"] == 250
   290	    ), f"Bar should hold updated volume, got {bars[0]['volume']}"
   291

   293	def test_ohlcv_query_timeframe_filter(client, admin_headers, tenant_with_admin):
   294	    """Querying bars returns only those matching the requested timeframe."""
   295	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   296	    ts = datetime(2024, 3, 1, 9, 15, 0).isoformat()
   297	    daily = {
   298	        "timeframe": "1d",
   299	        "timestamp": ts,
   300	        "open": 10.0,
   301	        "high": 11.0,
   302	        "low": 9.5,
   303	        "close": 10.5,
   304	        "volume": 500,
   305	    }
   306	    intraday = {
   307	        "timeframe": "1h",
   308	        "timestamp": ts,
   309	        "open": 10.0,
   310	        "high": 10.8,
   311	        "low": 9.8,
   312	        "close": 10.2,
   313	        "volume": 120,
   314	    }
   315	    upload = client.post(
   316	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",

   318	        headers=admin_headers,
   319	    )
   320	    assert upload.status_code == 201, f"OHLCV upload failed: {upload.text}"
   321	
   322	    # Default query (timeframe=1d) returns only the daily bar.
   323	    response = client.get(
   324	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   325	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   326	    )
   327	    assert response.status_code == 200, f"OHLCV query failed: {response.text}"
   328	    bars = response.json()
   329	    assert len(bars) == 1, f"1d query should return exactly one bar, got {len(bars)}"
   330	    assert (
   331	        bars[0]["timeframe"] == "1d"
   332	    ), f"Wrong timeframe returned: {bars[0]['timeframe']}"
   333	    assert bars[0]["close"] == 10.5, "Returned bar data mismatch"
   334	
   335	    hourly = client.get(
   336	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv?timeframe=1h",
   337	        headers=tenant_with_admin["headers"],
   338	    )
   339	    assert hourly.status_code == 200, f"1h query failed: {hourly.text}"
   340	    hourly_bars = hourly.json()
   341	    assert (
   342	        len(hourly_bars) == 1 and hourly_bars[0]["timeframe"] == "1h"
   343	    ), "1h query should return only the hourly bar"

   346	def test_ohlcv_query_date_range(client, admin_headers):
   347	    """Query with start/end filters returns only bars inside the range."""
   348	    instrument = _create_instrument(client, admin_headers)
   349	    base = datetime(2024, 5, 1, 0, 0, 0)
   350	    bars = [
   351	        {
   352	            "timeframe": "1d",
   353	            "timestamp": (base + timedelta(days=i)).isoformat(),
   354	            "open": 100.0,
   355	            "high": 101.0,
   356	            "low": 99.0,
   357	            "close": 100.5,
   358	            "volume": 10 + i,
   359	        }
   360	        for i in range(10)  # 2024-05-01 .. 2024-05-10
   361	    ]
   362	    upload = client.post(
   363	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv", json=bars, headers=admin_headers
   364	    )
   365	    assert upload.status_code == 201, f"OHLCV upload failed: {upload.text}"
   366	
   367	    params = {"start": "2024-05-03T00:00:00", "end": "2024-05-06T00:00:00"}
   368	    response = client.get(
   369	        f"/api/instruments/{instrument['id']}/ohlcv",
   370	        params=params,
   371	        headers=admin_headers,
   372	    )
   373	    assert response.status_code == 200, f"Date-range query failed: {response.text}"
   374	    bars_in_range = response.json()
   375	    timestamps = [b["timestamp"] for b in bars_in_range]
   376	    assert (
   377	        len(bars_in_range) == 4
   378	    ), f"Expected 4 bars in range, got {len(bars_in_range)}: {timestamps}"
   379	    assert all(
   380	        "2024-05-03" <= t[:10] <= "2024-05-06" for t in timestamps
   381	    ), f"Bars outside the requested range were returned: {timestamps}"
